# Core phase 1 implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the Rust core of the native line, sections 3.1 to 3.9 of the spec, as one crate with tests, plus the `anibeam-cli` binary, so that `anibeam-cli list` lists the real library identically to the Electron app.

**Architecture:** One crate, `anibeam-core`, holds every rule behind a synchronous facade: `Core::open`, `start`, `call`, `subscribe`, `shutdown`. Calls are plain enums that return within milliseconds; long work is a job on an internal tokio runtime that reports through events. Storage is one SQLite file behind a single writer thread; provider traffic goes through one rate-limited client per provider; secrets go to the keyring with a file fallback. The CLI links the crate in-process and opens the same database.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 2024 edition, tokio, rusqlite 0.40 (bundled SQLite), rusqlite_migration, notify 8.2 with notify-debouncer-full, reqwest 0.13 with rustls, governor, backon, keyring-core with the zbus Secret Service store, directories, serde, uniffi 0.32.0 derives, clap for the CLI.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-native-line-design.md`, chapter 3 (the core), with section 1 for the route and 4.1 for the parity checklist's core rules. The vocabulary is `CONTEXT.md` at the repository root: core, shell, bridge, call, reply, event, job, session, tick, view, mark, resume point, completion, skip window, track choice, subtitle defaults, source, series, match, missing, forget, export, private data, import. Use those words in code, comments, tests and commit messages, and none of the synonyms the glossary avoids.

Six digests of the Electron source were extracted while writing this plan and sit under the plan writer's scratchpad; every rule they carried is copied into the task that needs it, so this document and the spec are enough. Where a task says "carried from `src/...`", the Electron file is the tie-breaker if the plan and the spec disagree.

## Global constraints

Copied from the spec. Every task's requirements include this section.

- The native line lives beside the frozen Electron tree: `core/` for the crate, `apps/cli/` for the CLI, `apps/linux/` and `apps/macos/` later. `main` stays green for both worlds, so nothing here touches `src/`, `package.json` or the Electron build. Work on short branches off `main`, never on `main` directly; `bun run typecheck` must still pass before a merge.
- The native line starts at 2.0.0; until the `v2.0.0` tag exists the workspace version is `2.0.0-dev` and the describe string falls back to `CARGO_PKG_VERSION` when git is absent (spec 5.3).
- License: GPL-3.0-or-later. `LICENSE` lands at the root with the first native commit.
- Crate versions: rusqlite `0.40` with features `bundled`, `serde_json`, `backup`; rusqlite_migration `2.6`; notify `8.2` and notify-debouncer-full `0.7`; keyring-core `1.0` and zbus-secret-service-keyring-store `1.0` with feature `rt-tokio-crypto-rust`; reqwest `0.13` with rustls and the platform verifier, features `json` and `form`, no OpenSSL; governor `0.10`; backon `1.6`; directories `6`; uniffi pinned exactly at `=0.32.0`. Contract rule 10: uniffi is pinned exactly and bindings come from the same commit.
- Rust floor: `rust-version = "1.88"` (the highest floor among the pinned crates: notify 8.2 wants 1.77, notify-debouncer-full 0.7 1.85, keyring-core 1.85, zbus-secret-service-keyring-store 1.88, reqwest 1.85, backon 1.85, rusqlite_migration 1.84, time 1.88). The desktop runs rustc 1.92.0 on rustup stable. This settles spec 5.5's "Rust toolchain floor".
- Contract constraints 1 to 10 (spec 3.1): `Call`, `Reply`, `Event`, `EventBody` and `CoreError` are plain enums with named-field variants; every record is a struct with named fields; all derive `Clone`, `Debug`, `serde::Serialize`, `serde::Deserialize` and the uniffi derive. Field types come from the closed set: `bool`, `u8` to `u64`, `i8` to `i64`, `f32`, `f64`, `String`, `Vec<u8>`, `SystemTime`, `Duration`, `Option<T>`, `Vec<T>`, `HashMap<K, V>`, records, enums, `Box<T>`. Never `usize`, `u128`, `char`, tuples, arrays, generics, references, `PathBuf`, `Url`, `Uuid`. Counts and ids are `u64`, paths are `String`, seconds are `f64`, instants are `SystemTime`. No `Arc<Object>` inside any of them. The core is one `Arc<Core>`, `Send + Sync`, `&self` methods only, std locks held briefly, tokio inside behind `Runtime::spawn`, nothing exported is `async`. No `#[cfg(target_os = ...)]` on any exported item. No exported code panics on shell input.
- A call returns within milliseconds off memory or the database; anything that touches the disk at scale, the network or a child process is a job that replies `Started { job }`. Jobs of kind Scan, AutoMatch, RefreshAll, Crawl, RefreshProgress, RefreshWatching, FillImages and Subscriptions run one at a time per kind; a second call replies `Started` with the running job's id. `CancelJob` ends any job with a terminal `JobCancelled`. A call fails synchronously only on its own arguments and preconditions.
- The core coalesces: `SeriesChanged` carries a batch, `JobProgress` fires at most four times a second per job, no event is ever per file. The activity log is signal-only: an event is a state change, and the core writes its line once.
- Storage: one SQLite file `<data_dir>/anibeam.db`; every connection sets `journal_mode=WAL`, `synchronous=NORMAL`, `foreign_keys=ON` and a five second `busy_timeout`; one writer connection on its own std thread; every multi-table write is one `TransactionBehavior::Immediate` transaction; `shutdown` runs `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`. Instants are integer unix seconds in the database and `SystemTime` at the bridge; positions are `REAL` seconds; provider dates stay `YYYY-MM-DD` text. A migration that has reached `main` is never edited.
- Paths are XDG paths named `anibeam`: data `~/.local/share/anibeam/` (the database and `secrets.json`), config `~/.config/anibeam/`, cache `~/.cache/anibeam/` (`images/`), state `~/.local/state/anibeam/`.
- No polling, ever: no intervals, no periodic rescans, no `PollWatcher`. The launch catch-up walk is the only whole-library read the core does on its own.
- Every provider call goes through the per-provider limiter (AniList 800 ms gap, Jikan 1100 ms gap, AniSkip and MAL as fixed below), with backon's 429 schedule 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 s capped at 60, six attempts, `Retry-After` honoured. Jobs walk their work one series at a time.
- Secrets never enter the database. Keyring calls run on a blocking thread, never inside a tokio task.
- TMDB is out of the native line: no key, no client, no search; a carried match keeps `provider`, `tmdb_id`, `tmdb_kind` and nothing else.
- MAL is not a matching provider; Jikan is only the per-episode side-fetch by MAL id.
- Episode thumbnails are never rendered or fetched.
- House style for everything written: no em dashes, no en dashes, sentence case headings, no decorative emoji, plain words. Commit messages follow `type(scope): summary`, for example `feat(core): the scan job reconciles files with disk`. Never add a `Co-Authored-By` line.

## Decisions this plan makes

The spec's "Left open" lists are questions for phase 1 to settle in code. Each is settled here so an implementer never has to guess; the task that implements each one repeats the decision.

| Section | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| 3.1 | Reply variants positional or named | Named fields on every variant, so `Reply` matches constraint 1 and its JSON reads like every other enum: `{"Sources":{"sources":[...]}}`. |
| 3.1 | Crate and namespace names | The crate is `anibeam-core` (lib `anibeam_core`), never `core`, which collides with Rust's own crate. The uniffi namespace is the crate name, so the Mac appendix's `libcore.so` is `libanibeam_core.so`. The CLI is the package `anibeam-cli` at `apps/cli/`. |
| 3.1 | The `match` field on `SeriesCard` | Named `match_info` in code, since `match` is a Rust keyword and raw identifiers do not survive the bridges. |
| 3.3 | What `ScanFinished.removed` counts | The series the scan marked missing. `RemoveSource` has its own events and no `ScanFinished`. |
| 3.4 | `RefreshSeries` on an unmatched series | Replies `Err(Refused { reason: Unmatched })` at once; the Match button is the recovery. The `Unmatched` refusal exists in the contract for exactly this. |
| 3.4 | `ApplyMatch` with `MatchTarget::Mal { id }` | The job resolves the id through AniList's `Media(idMal:)` query. Found: the normal AniList path, both ids on the row. Not found: a MAL-only confirmed match, `provider = mal`, `mal_id` set, no media row, title from the folder, no poster, left alone by auto-match. `ResolveLink` keeps failing with a message when AniList has no entry, so only a deliberate `Mal` target produces a MAL-only match. |
| 3.4 | Widen the AniList gap to 2 s | No. 800 ms stays; a 429 under the degraded 30 per minute limit goes through the `Retry-After` path. |
| 3.5 | Mark, progress or score with no tracker connected | `Err(NotConnected { tracker })` at once, naming the main tracker. |
| 3.5 | Credentials after a disconnect | They survive. Disconnect keeps the row's `client_id` and the client secret in the keyring, and clears `user_id`, `username`, `expires_at`, `connected_at`, `synced_at`, `progress_fetched_at`, the entries and the tokens. `connected` is `connected_at IS NOT NULL`. |
| 3.5 | A governor period for MAL's list API | Yes, one limiter for MAL at a 500 ms gap; 429 is the signal and goes through the same backon path. |
| 3.5 | The AniList pin redirect | Not in phase 1. No call and no field; the loopback listener is the only path. Noted for a shell ticket if the port is ever blocked. |
| 3.6 | `GraphNode.w` and `h` | 180 by 420 layout units, Electron's card, so the positions and the card agree; the shell scales. |
| 3.6 | Where an edge attaches | The shell's; the core carries positions only. |
| 3.6 | Ghost copies and side-story frames | None. One node per AniList id, one edge per `(from, to, relation)`. |
| 3.6 | Drawing a node whose relations are owed | `GraphNode` gains `pending: bool`, true while `relations_fetched_at` is NULL or `crawl_deferred_until` is in the future. Adding a field is allowed by the contract. |
| 3.6 | Which node anchors the rows | The schema's root, the smallest member id. Electron's earliest-released rule is dropped. |
| 3.6 | The AniList address of an unowned node | `GraphNode` gains `site_url: Option<String>` from `anilist_media.site_url`; the shell falls back to `https://anilist.co/anime/<id>`. |
| 3.7 | The watching list's staleness window | Every `ListWatching` starts a refresh when none is running, as Electron did on every visit. |
| 3.7 | The feed's reason for a film | `Downloaded { at }` from the file's `mtime`, which is never NULL. |
| 3.7 | The address of an external watching card | `WatchingEntry` gains `site_url: Option<String>`; the shell falls back to `https://anilist.co/anime/<id>`. |
| 3.7 | An external card's countdown and score | `WatchingEntry` gains `next_airing: Option<Airing>` and `score: Option<f64>`, both from the query the refresh already sends. |
| 3.7 | `updated_at` when AniList sends none | Stored as NULL and carried as `SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH`, so the entry sorts last and the shell shows nothing for the epoch. |
| 3.8 | Skip windows for an extra | `ReportChapters` on an extra's session answers from chapters only, never AniSkip, since an extra has no episode number; the answer is cached under the extra's key like any other. Auto-skip is the shell's. |
| 3.9 | `exportedBy.line` in a native export | `native`. |
| 3.9 | Where the export carries `track_choice` and `auto_skip` | Version 2 of `anibeam-export`: version 1 plus `series[].trackChoice` (a `TrackChoice` object or null, present in both variants since it describes files) and `preferences.autoSkip { intro, outro }`. The core reads 1 and 2 and writes 2; `supported` in the `Version` error is 2. |
| 3.9 | Does completion emit `SeriesChanged` | Yes, carrying the card, so a series page behind the player re-reads Next up. |
| 3.9 | A series whose path lies under none of the file's sources | It attaches to whichever source, existing or imported, contains its path (longest prefix). With none, the entry is skipped and counted under `fields_ignored` as `series[<n>].path`. |

Test conventions used throughout:

- Unit tests live beside the code in `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`. Integration tests under `core/tests/` open a `Core` on a `tempfile::tempdir()` with `CorePaths` pointing at it; they never touch the real XDG directories or the real keyring.
- Anything that needs the network takes an `Arc<dyn Http>`; tests pass `FakeHttp` (Task 13) with canned replies and assert on the recorded requests.
- Anything that depends on the clock takes `now: SystemTime` as an argument; the `Core` passes `time::now()`.
- Run one crate's tests with `cargo test -p anibeam-core`; run one test with `cargo test -p anibeam-core <name>`. Every task ends with `cargo test -p anibeam-core` green and `cargo clippy -p anibeam-core --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean.

### Task 1: Workspace, the core crate, errors, paths and the version string

**Files:**
- Create: `Cargo.toml`
- Create: `LICENSE`
- Create: `core/Cargo.toml`
- Create: `core/build.rs`
- Create: `core/src/lib.rs`
- Create: `core/src/paths.rs`
- Create: `core/src/time.rs`
- Create: `core/src/contract/mod.rs`
- Create: `core/src/contract/error.rs`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (add `/target/`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces: `CoreError` with variants `NotFound { what: Entity, id: u64 }`, `Invalid { field, message }`, `Unavailable { path }`, `NotConnected { tracker: Tracker }`, `Refused { reason: Refusal }`, `Provider { provider, status: Option<u32>, message, retry_after: Option<f64> }`, `Io { path: Option<String>, message }`, `Storage { message }`, `Keyring { message }`, `Unsupported { what }`, `Version { found: u32, supported: u32 }`, `Internal { message }`; `Entity { Source, Series, File, Session, Job }`; `Refusal { Hidden, NoMatch, NotNewer, Extra, Unmatched, OnDisk }`; `CorePaths { data_dir, config_dir, cache_dir, state_dir }` with `CorePaths::xdg()`, `db_path()`, `secrets_path()`, `images_dir()`; `anibeam_core::VERSION: &str`; `time::now_secs() -> i64`, `time::to_secs(SystemTime) -> i64`, `time::from_secs(i64) -> SystemTime`.

`CoreError` needs `Tracker` and `Provider`, so this task creates `contract/enums.rs` with only those two; Task 2 fills the rest of that file.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests for the version string, the paths and the error type**

`core/src/lib.rs`:

```rust
//! AniBeam's core. One crate, every rule; every shell uses it through the
//! contract in `contract`.

pub mod contract;
pub mod paths;
pub mod time;

pub use contract::*;
pub use paths::CorePaths;

/// The describe string computed by build.rs: `2.0.0.r14.g1a2b3c4` at a commit,
/// `CARGO_PKG_VERSION` when git is absent. The shell and the CLI print it.
pub const VERSION: &str = env!("ANIBEAM_VERSION");

uniffi::setup_scaffolding!();

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[test]
    fn version_is_not_empty() {
        assert!(!super::VERSION.is_empty());
    }
}
```

`core/src/paths.rs` test block:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn xdg_paths_end_in_anibeam() {
        let p = CorePaths::xdg().unwrap();
        for dir in [&p.data_dir, &p.config_dir, &p.cache_dir, &p.state_dir] {
            assert!(dir.ends_with("/anibeam"), "{dir}");
        }
        assert!(p.db_path().ends_with("anibeam/anibeam.db"));
        assert!(p.secrets_path().ends_with("anibeam/secrets.json"));
        assert!(p.images_dir().ends_with("anibeam/images"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn paths_for_a_directory_put_everything_under_it() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let p = CorePaths::under(dir.path());
        assert_eq!(p.db_path(), dir.path().join("data").join("anibeam.db"));
        assert_eq!(p.images_dir(), dir.path().join("cache").join("images"));
    }
}
```

`core/src/contract/error.rs` test block:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn errors_serialise_externally_tagged() {
        let e = CoreError::NotFound { what: Entity::Series, id: 7 };
        let json = serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(json, serde_json::json!({ "NotFound": { "what": "Series", "id": 7 } }));
        let back: CoreError = serde_json::from_value(json).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(back, e);
    }

    #[test]
    fn storage_errors_convert_from_rusqlite() {
        let e: CoreError = rusqlite::Error::QueryReturnedNoRows.into();
        assert!(matches!(e, CoreError::Storage { .. }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn display_names_the_thing() {
        let e = CoreError::Refused { reason: Refusal::OnDisk };
        assert_eq!(e.to_string(), "refused: OnDisk");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write build.rs, paths, time and the error type**

`core/build.rs`:

```rust
use std::process::Command;

fn main() {
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=../.git/HEAD");
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=../.git/refs/tags");
    let fallback = std::env::var("CARGO_PKG_VERSION").unwrap_or_default();
    let describe = Command::new("git")
        .args(["describe", "--tags", "--dirty"])
        .output()
        .ok()
        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
        .and_then(|o| String::from_utf8(o.stdout).ok())
        .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty());
    // The same rewrite the PKGBUILD's pkgver() applies: v2.0.0-14-g1a2b3c4-dirty
    // becomes 2.0.0.r14.g1a2b3c4.dirty, so the binary and pacman agree.
    let version = describe
        .map(|d| {
            let d = d.strip_prefix('v').unwrap_or(&d).to_string();
            let d = d.replace("-dirty", ".dirty");
            let d = match d.find("-g") {
                Some(i) => {
                    let (head, tail) = d.split_at(i);
                    let head = match head.rfind('-') {
                        Some(j) => format!("{}.r{}", &head[..j], &head[j + 1..]),
                        None => head.to_string(),
                    };
                    format!("{head}.{}", &tail[1..])
                }
                None => d,
            };
            d.replace('-', ".")
        })
        .unwrap_or(fallback);
    println!("cargo:rustc-env=ANIBEAM_VERSION={version}");
}
```

`core/src/time.rs`:

```rust
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

pub fn now() -> SystemTime {
    SystemTime::now()
}

pub fn now_secs() -> i64 {
    to_secs(now())
}

/// Unix seconds, the database's instant column type.
pub fn to_secs(t: SystemTime) -> i64 {
    match t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH) {
        Ok(d) => d.as_secs() as i64,
        Err(e) => -(e.duration().as_secs() as i64),
    }
}

pub fn from_secs(s: i64) -> SystemTime {
    if s >= 0 {
        UNIX_EPOCH + Duration::from_secs(s as u64)
    } else {
        UNIX_EPOCH - Duration::from_secs((-s) as u64)
    }
}

pub fn opt_from_secs(s: Option<i64>) -> Option<SystemTime> {
    s.map(from_secs)
}

pub fn opt_to_secs(t: Option<SystemTime>) -> Option<i64> {
    t.map(to_secs)
}
```

`core/src/paths.rs`:

```rust
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use directories::ProjectDirs;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::contract::CoreError;

/// Where the core keeps its files. Every field is a directory path as a
/// String, per the contract's closed type set; the accessors return PathBuf
/// for the code inside the crate.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Record)]
pub struct CorePaths {
    pub data_dir: String,
    pub config_dir: String,
    pub cache_dir: String,
    pub state_dir: String,
}

impl CorePaths {
    /// The XDG directories named `anibeam`, which on Linux are
    /// ~/.local/share/anibeam, ~/.config/anibeam, ~/.cache/anibeam and
    /// ~/.local/state/anibeam.
    pub fn xdg() -> Result<CorePaths, CoreError> {
        let dirs = ProjectDirs::from("", "", "anibeam").ok_or_else(|| CoreError::Io {
            path: None,
            message: "no home directory".to_string(),
        })?;
        let state = dirs
            .state_dir()
            .map(Path::to_path_buf)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| dirs.data_local_dir().join("state"));
        Ok(CorePaths {
            data_dir: dirs.data_local_dir().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            config_dir: dirs.config_dir().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            cache_dir: dirs.cache_dir().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            state_dir: state.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
        })
    }

    /// Every directory under one root: data/, config/, cache/, state/. Tests
    /// and the CLI's --root flag use it.
    pub fn under(root: &Path) -> CorePaths {
        CorePaths {
            data_dir: root.join("data").to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            config_dir: root.join("config").to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            cache_dir: root.join("cache").to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            state_dir: root.join("state").to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
        }
    }

    pub fn db_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        Path::new(&self.data_dir).join("anibeam.db")
    }

    pub fn secrets_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        Path::new(&self.data_dir).join("secrets.json")
    }

    pub fn images_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
        Path::new(&self.cache_dir).join("images")
    }
}
```

```rust
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Provider {
    Anilist,
    Mal,
    Tmdb,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Tracker {
    Anilist,
    Mal,
}

impl Tracker {
    /// The value every `tracker` column holds.
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Tracker::Anilist => "anilist",
            Tracker::Mal => "mal",
        }
    }

    pub fn from_str(s: &str) -> Option<Tracker> {
        match s {
            "anilist" => Some(Tracker::Anilist),
            "mal" => Some(Tracker::Mal),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// The label a user reads: AniList or MAL.
    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Tracker::Anilist => "AniList",
            Tracker::Mal => "MAL",
        }
    }
}

impl Provider {
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Provider::Anilist => "anilist",
            Provider::Mal => "mal",
            Provider::Tmdb => "tmdb",
        }
    }

    pub fn from_str(s: &str) -> Option<Provider> {
        match s {
            "anilist" => Some(Provider::Anilist),
            "mal" => Some(Provider::Mal),
            "tmdb" => Some(Provider::Tmdb),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}
```

`core/src/contract/error.rs`:

```rust
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use super::{Provider, Tracker};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Entity {
    Source,
    Series,
    File,
    Session,
    Job,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Refusal {
    Hidden,
    NoMatch,
    NotNewer,
    Extra,
    Unmatched,
    OnDisk,
}

/// One enum, every fallible call returns it, and no exported code panics on
/// shell input. Third-party errors arrive as the message string.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize, thiserror::Error, uniffi::Error)]
pub enum CoreError {
    #[error("{what:?} {id} not found")]
    NotFound { what: Entity, id: u64 },
    #[error("invalid {field}: {message}")]
    Invalid { field: String, message: String },
    #[error("source path unavailable: {path}")]
    Unavailable { path: String },
    #[error("{tracker:?} is not connected")]
    NotConnected { tracker: Tracker },
    #[error("refused: {reason:?}")]
    Refused { reason: Refusal },
    #[error("{provider:?}: {message}")]
    Provider { provider: Provider, status: Option<u32>, message: String, retry_after: Option<f64> },
    #[error("io: {message}")]
    Io { path: Option<String>, message: String },
    #[error("storage: {message}")]
    Storage { message: String },
    #[error("keyring: {message}")]
    Keyring { message: String },
    #[error("unsupported: {what}")]
    Unsupported { what: String },
    #[error("export version {found} is newer than the supported {supported}")]
    Version { found: u32, supported: u32 },
    #[error("internal: {message}")]
    Internal { message: String },
}

impl From<rusqlite::Error> for CoreError {
    fn from(e: rusqlite::Error) -> Self {
        CoreError::Storage { message: e.to_string() }
    }
}

impl From<rusqlite_migration::Error> for CoreError {
    fn from(e: rusqlite_migration::Error) -> Self {
        CoreError::Storage { message: e.to_string() }
    }
}

impl From<std::io::Error> for CoreError {
    fn from(e: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        CoreError::Io { path: None, message: e.to_string() }
    }
}

impl From<serde_json::Error> for CoreError {
    fn from(e: serde_json::Error) -> Self {
        CoreError::Internal { message: format!("json: {e}") }
    }
}

impl CoreError {
    pub fn io_at(path: impl Into<String>, e: std::io::Error) -> CoreError {
        CoreError::Io { path: Some(path.into()), message: e.to_string() }
    }

    pub fn internal(message: impl Into<String>) -> CoreError {
        CoreError::Internal { message: message.into() }
    }

    pub fn invalid(field: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> CoreError {
        CoreError::Invalid { field: field.to_string(), message: message.into() }
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::time::UNIX_EPOCH;

    fn assert_send_sync<T: Send + Sync + Clone + std::fmt::Debug>() {}

    #[test]
    fn contract_types_are_send_sync_clone_debug() {
        assert_send_sync::<Call>();
        assert_send_sync::<Reply>();
        assert_send_sync::<Event>();
        assert_send_sync::<EventBody>();
        assert_send_sync::<CoreError>();
        assert_send_sync::<SeriesCard>();
        assert_send_sync::<PlaybackSession>();
    }

    #[test]
    fn calls_round_trip_through_json_externally_tagged() {
        let call = Call::MarkEpisode { series: 3, episode: 12.0 };
        let json = serde_json::to_value(&call).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(json, serde_json::json!({ "MarkEpisode": { "series": 3, "episode": 12.0 } }));
        let back: Call = serde_json::from_value(json).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(back, call);
        let unit: Call = serde_json::from_value(serde_json::json!("ListSources")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unit, Call::ListSources);
    }

    #[test]
    fn replies_have_named_fields() {
        let reply = Reply::Started { job: 9 };
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&reply).unwrap(), serde_json::json!({ "Started": { "job": 9 } }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn events_round_trip() {
        let event = Event {
            seq: 1,
            at: UNIX_EPOCH,
            level: Level::Info,
            stage: Stage::Library,
            message: "scan finished".into(),
            job: Some(JobRef { id: 4, kind: JobKind::Scan, phase: JobPhase::Finished }),
            body: EventBody::ScanFinished { source: None, added: 2, changed: 0, removed: 1 },
        };
        let json = serde_json::to_string(&event).unwrap();
        let back: Event = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(back, event);
    }

    #[test]
    fn job_kinds_know_their_stage_and_serialisation() {
        assert_eq!(JobKind::Scan.stage(), Stage::Library);
        assert_eq!(JobKind::Crawl.stage(), Stage::Franchise);
        assert!(JobKind::Scan.one_at_a_time());
        assert!(!JobKind::Search.one_at_a_time());
        assert_eq!(JobKind::FillImages.one_at_a_time(), true);
    }

    #[test]
    fn subtitle_defaults_are_mpvs_stock_values() {
        let d = SubtitleDefaults::default();
        assert_eq!(d.subtitle_languages, vec!["en".to_string()]);
        assert_eq!(d.audio_languages, vec!["ja".to_string()]);
        assert_eq!(d.scale, 1.0);
        assert_eq!(d.ass_override, AssOverride::ScaleOnly);
        assert_eq!(d.text_style.font, "sans-serif");
        assert_eq!(d.text_style.colour, Colour { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255, a: 255 });
        assert_eq!(d.text_style.outline_size, 1.65);
        assert_eq!(d.text_style.outline_colour, Colour { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 255 });
        assert_eq!(d.text_style.position, 100.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn preferences_default_to_electrons_defaults() {
        let p = Preferences::default();
        assert_eq!(p.title_language, TitleLanguage::Romaji);
        assert_eq!(p.library_tab, Tab::All);
        assert_eq!(p.library_sort, Sort::Alpha);
        assert_eq!(p.library_direction, Direction::Asc);
        assert_eq!(p.feed_sort, FeedSort::Recent);
    }
}
```

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum SeriesKind { Show, Movie }              // series.kind: 'show' | 'movie'

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum TmdbKind { Tv, Movie }                  // 'tv' | 'movie'

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum AiringStatus { Releasing, Finished, NotYetReleased, Cancelled, Hiatus }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum WatchedState { Behind, CaughtUp, Unknown }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum ListStatus { Watching, Planning, Completed, Paused, Dropped, Repeating }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Tab { All, Series, Movies, Hidden }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Sort { Alpha, LastViewed, Progress, CommunityScore, MyScore }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Direction { Asc, Desc }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum FeedSort { Recent, Upcoming }           // Recently released, Coming soon; export 'recent' | 'upcoming'

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum MetadataFilter { All, Series, Movies, MissingFiles }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum ExtraKind { Op, Ed, Pv, Sp, Other }     // 'op' | 'ed' | 'pv' | 'sp' | 'other'

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum SkipKind { Intro, Outro }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum SkipSource { Chapters, AniSkip }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum TitleLanguage { Romaji, English }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum AssOverride { AsScripted, ScaleOnly, Force }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum TrackKind { Embedded, Sidecar }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum CloseReason { Ended, Stopped, Switched }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Level { Debug, Info, Warn, Error }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum Stage { Library, Metadata, Trackers, Franchise, Playback, Store, System }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum JobPhase { Started, Running, Finished }

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum JobKind {
    Scan, AutoMatch, Search, ResolveLink, ApplyMatch, Refresh, RefreshAll, RefreshAiring,
    ClearImages, FillImages, ConnectTracker, Mark, SetProgress, Score, RefreshProgress,
    RefreshWatching, Crawl, SkipWindows, Export, Import, Subscriptions,
}

impl JobKind {
    pub fn stage(self) -> Stage {
        use JobKind::*;
        match self {
            Scan | Subscriptions => Stage::Library,
            AutoMatch | Search | ResolveLink | ApplyMatch | Refresh | RefreshAll | RefreshAiring
            | ClearImages | FillImages => Stage::Metadata,
            ConnectTracker | Mark | SetProgress | Score | RefreshProgress | RefreshWatching => Stage::Trackers,
            Crawl => Stage::Franchise,
            SkipWindows => Stage::Playback,
            Export | Import => Stage::Store,
        }
    }

    /// Kinds that run one at a time: a second call replies Started with the running id.
    pub fn one_at_a_time(self) -> bool {
        use JobKind::*;
        matches!(self, Scan | AutoMatch | RefreshAll | Crawl | RefreshProgress | RefreshWatching | FillImages | Subscriptions)
    }
}
```

```rust
use std::time::SystemTime;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Record)]
pub struct JobRef { pub id: u64, pub kind: JobKind, pub phase: JobPhase }

/// Every event is one envelope. `message` is the activity log line, written
/// by the core once; the shell never composes log text.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Record)]
pub struct Event {
    pub seq: u64,
    pub at: SystemTime,
    pub level: Level,
    pub stage: Stage,
    pub message: String,
    pub job: Option<JobRef>,
    pub body: EventBody,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize, uniffi::Enum)]
pub enum EventBody {
    Ready,
    Notice,
    JobStarted { kind: JobKind },
    JobProgress { done: u64, total: Option<u64>, label: String },
    JobFailed { error: CoreError },
    JobCancelled,
    SourceChanged { source: Source },
    SourceRemoved { source: u64 },
    SeriesChanged { series: Vec<SeriesCard> },
    SeriesRemoved { ids: Vec<u64> },
    ScanFinished { source: Option<u64>, added: u64, changed: u64, removed: u64 },
    SubscriptionsListed { result: SubscriptionsResult },
    SearchFinished { results: Vec<SearchResult> },
    LinkResolved { target: MatchTarget },
    MatchApplied { series: u64 },
    RefreshFinished { refreshed: u64, failed: u64 },
    AutoMatchFinished { backfilled: u64, matched: u64, unmatched: u64 },
    AiringRefreshed { series: u64, updated: bool },
    ImagesCleared { removed: u64 },
    TrackersChanged { state: TrackerState },
    AuthUrlReady { tracker: Tracker, open_url: String, redirect_url: String },
    TrackerConnected { tracker: Tracker, username: String },
    Marked { series: u64, episode: u32, outcomes: Vec<TrackerOutcome> },
    ProgressSet { series: u64, progress: u32, outcomes: Vec<TrackerOutcome> },
    Scored { series: u64, score: Option<f64>, outcomes: Vec<TrackerOutcome> },
    ProgressRefreshed { tracker: Tracker },
    WatchingRefreshed { list: WatchingList },
    GraphChanged { root: u64 },
    CrawlFinished { fetched: u64, deferred: u64 },
    SkipWindowsReady { session: u64, windows: Vec<SkipWindow> },
    ResumePointChanged { file: u64, position: Option<f64> },
    Viewed { series: u64, episode: String },
    PreferencesChanged { preferences: Preferences },
    SettingsChanged,
    ExportFinished { path: String },
    ImportFinished { summary: ImportSummary },
}

/// The listener a shell hands to `subscribe`. Called by value on whatever
/// tokio thread produced the event; the shell owns the hop to its UI thread.
#[uniffi::export(foreign)]
pub trait EventListener: Send + Sync {
    fn on_event(&self, event: Event);
}
```

`Viewed.episode` carries the episode key (`12`, `12.5`, or a film's file name), since a film can be viewed and has no number. `Marked.episode` is the floored integer the trackers received.

### Task 3: The store: one file, one writer thread, migration 1

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/store/mod.rs`
- Create: `core/src/store/migrations.rs`
- Create: `core/src/store/sql/0001_initial.sql`
- Create: `core/src/store/settings.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/lib.rs` (add `pub mod store;`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CoreError`, `CorePaths`, `time::*`.
- Produces:
  - `Store::open(db_path: &Path) -> Result<Arc<Store>, CoreError>`: creates the directory, spawns the writer thread, applies pragmas and migrations, refuses a newer `user_version`.
  - `Store::write<T, F>(&self, f: F) -> Result<T, CoreError>` where `F: FnOnce(&mut rusqlite::Connection) -> Result<T, CoreError> + Send + 'static`, `T: Send + 'static`: runs `f` on the writer thread and waits for the result. Used for every call's reads and writes.
  - `Store::tx<T, F>(&self, f: F) -> Result<T, CoreError>` where `F: FnOnce(&rusqlite::Transaction) -> Result<T, CoreError> + Send + 'static`: `write` wrapped in one IMMEDIATE transaction, committed on `Ok`, rolled back on `Err`.
  - `Store::reader(&self) -> Result<rusqlite::Connection, CoreError>`: a fresh read connection with the same pragmas, for long reads inside jobs.
  - `Store::checkpoint(&self)`: `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`.
  - `settings::get<T: DeserializeOwned>(conn, key) -> Result<Option<T>, CoreError>` and `settings::set<T: Serialize>(conn, key, &T) -> Result<(), CoreError>`, with the key constants `PREFERENCES`, `SUBTITLE_DEFAULTS`, `AUTO_SKIP`, `MAIN_TRACKER`, `AUTO_MATCH_VERSION`, `WATCHING_FETCHED_AT`.
  - `migrations::MIGRATIONS` and `migrations::SCHEMA_VERSION: usize` (the count, private to the crate, so the `usize` never crosses the contract).

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn open_temp() -> (tempfile::TempDir, Arc<Store>) {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let store = Store::open(&dir.path().join("data").join("anibeam.db")).unwrap();
        (dir, store)
    }

    #[test]
    fn open_creates_the_directory_and_sets_wal() {
        let (dir, store) = open_temp();
        assert!(dir.path().join("data").join("anibeam.db").exists());
        let mode: String = store.write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("PRAGMA journal_mode", [], |r| r.get(0))?)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(mode, "wal");
        let fk: i64 = store.write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("PRAGMA foreign_keys", [], |r| r.get(0))?)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fk, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn migrations_validate_and_reach_the_latest_version() {
        migrations::MIGRATIONS.validate().unwrap();
        let (_dir, store) = open_temp();
        let v: i64 = store.write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("PRAGMA user_version", [], |r| r.get(0))?)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v as usize, migrations::SCHEMA_VERSION);
        let tables: i64 = store
            .write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name IN ('sources','series','files','anilist_media','anilist_episodes','recommendations','relations','tracker_accounts','tracker_entries','views','completed','resume_points','skip_windows','settings','images','events')", [], |r| r.get(0))?))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(tables, 16);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_newer_database_is_refused_naming_both_versions() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("anibeam.db");
        {
            let c = rusqlite::Connection::open(&path).unwrap();
            c.pragma_update(None, "user_version", 99).unwrap();
        }
        let err = Store::open(&path).err().unwrap();
        match err {
            CoreError::Storage { message } => {
                assert!(message.contains("99"), "{message}");
                assert!(message.contains(&migrations::SCHEMA_VERSION.to_string()), "{message}");
            }
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn tx_rolls_back_on_error_and_commits_on_ok() {
        let (_dir, store) = open_temp();
        let err = store.tx(|t| {
            t.execute("INSERT INTO sources (path, added_at) VALUES ('/a', 1)", [])?;
            Err::<(), _>(CoreError::internal("boom"))
        });
        assert!(err.is_err());
        let n: i64 = store.write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM sources", [], |r| r.get(0))?)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(n, 0);
        store.tx(|t| { t.execute("INSERT INTO sources (path, added_at) VALUES ('/a', 1)", [])?; Ok(()) }).unwrap();
        let n: i64 = store.write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM sources", [], |r| r.get(0))?)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(n, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn readers_see_committed_writes() {
        let (_dir, store) = open_temp();
        store.tx(|t| { t.execute("INSERT INTO sources (path, added_at) VALUES ('/b', 1)", [])?; Ok(()) }).unwrap();
        let reader = store.reader().unwrap();
        let n: i64 = reader.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM sources", [], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(n, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn settings_round_trip_json() {
        let (_dir, store) = open_temp();
        store.write(|c| settings::set(c, settings::AUTO_MATCH_VERSION, &3u32)).unwrap();
        let v: Option<u32> = store.write(|c| settings::get(c, settings::AUTO_MATCH_VERSION)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v, Some(3));
        let missing: Option<u32> = store.write(|c| settings::get(c, "nope")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(missing, None);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the migration list and the store**

`core/src/store/migrations.rs`:

```rust
use rusqlite::Connection;
use rusqlite_migration::{M, Migrations};
use std::sync::LazyLock;

use crate::contract::CoreError;

/// Each migration is one SQL file under sql/, listed in order. A migration
/// that has reached main is never edited; a fix is the next migration.
pub static MIGRATIONS: LazyLock<Migrations<'static>> = LazyLock::new(|| {
    Migrations::new(vec![
        M::up(include_str!("sql/0001_initial.sql")).foreign_key_check(),
    ])
});

pub const SCHEMA_VERSION: usize = 1;

/// Refuses a database written by a newer build, then applies what is pending
/// inside rusqlite_migration's own transaction.
pub fn apply(conn: &mut Connection) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
    let found: i64 = conn.query_row("PRAGMA user_version", [], |r| r.get(0))?;
    if found as usize > SCHEMA_VERSION {
        return Err(CoreError::Storage {
            message: format!(
                "database schema version {found} is newer than this build's {SCHEMA_VERSION}; refusing to open it"
            ),
        });
    }
    MIGRATIONS.to_latest(conn)?;
    Ok(())
}
```

`core/src/store/mod.rs`:

```rust
pub mod migrations;
pub mod settings;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Sender};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::thread::JoinHandle;
use std::time::Duration;

use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags, Transaction, TransactionBehavior};

use crate::contract::CoreError;

type Task = Box<dyn FnOnce(&mut Connection) + Send + 'static>;

/// One SQLite file behind one writer connection on its own thread. Every
/// call's reads and writes go through `write`; jobs open a `reader` for
/// long reads. Connection is Send and not Sync, so only the writer thread
/// ever touches the writer connection.
pub struct Store {
    path: PathBuf,
    tx: Mutex<Option<Sender<Task>>>,
    thread: Mutex<Option<JoinHandle<()>>>,
}

fn apply_pragmas(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
    let mode: String = conn.pragma_update_and_check(None, "journal_mode", "WAL", |r| r.get(0))?;
    if mode.to_lowercase() != "wal" {
        return Err(CoreError::Storage { message: format!("journal_mode is {mode}, not wal; is the data directory on a network filesystem?") });
    }
    conn.pragma_update(None, "synchronous", "NORMAL")?;
    conn.pragma_update(None, "foreign_keys", "ON")?;
    conn.busy_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))?;
    Ok(())
}

impl Store {
    pub fn open(db_path: &Path) -> Result<Arc<Store>, CoreError> {
        if let Some(parent) = db_path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| CoreError::io_at(parent.to_string_lossy(), e))?;
        }
        let mut conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;
        apply_pragmas(&conn)?;
        migrations::apply(&mut conn)?;

        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<Task>();
        let thread = std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("anibeam-store".into())
            .spawn(move || {
                let mut conn = conn;
                while let Ok(task) = rx.recv() {
                    task(&mut conn);
                }
                let _ = conn.execute_batch("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)");
            })
            .map_err(|e| CoreError::internal(format!("store thread: {e}")))?;

        Ok(Arc::new(Store { path: db_path.to_path_buf(), tx: Mutex::new(Some(tx)), thread: Mutex::new(Some(thread)) }))
    }

    pub fn path(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.path
    }

    pub fn write<T, F>(&self, f: F) -> Result<T, CoreError>
    where
        T: Send + 'static,
        F: FnOnce(&mut Connection) -> Result<T, CoreError> + Send + 'static,
    {
        let (rtx, rrx) = mpsc::channel::<Result<T, CoreError>>();
        let sender = self.tx.lock().unwrap().clone().ok_or_else(|| CoreError::internal("store is closed"))?;
        sender
            .send(Box::new(move |conn| {
                let _ = rtx.send(f(conn));
            }))
            .map_err(|_| CoreError::internal("store thread is gone"))?;
        rrx.recv().map_err(|_| CoreError::internal("store thread dropped the reply"))?
    }

    pub fn tx<T, F>(&self, f: F) -> Result<T, CoreError>
    where
        T: Send + 'static,
        F: FnOnce(&Transaction) -> Result<T, CoreError> + Send + 'static,
    {
        self.write(move |conn| {
            let tx = conn.transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
            let out = f(&tx)?;
            tx.commit()?;
            Ok(out)
        })
    }

    pub fn reader(&self) -> Result<Connection, CoreError> {
        let conn = Connection::open_with_flags(&self.path, OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_NO_MUTEX)?;
        conn.pragma_update(None, "foreign_keys", "ON")?;
        conn.busy_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))?;
        Ok(conn)
    }

    pub fn checkpoint(&self) {
        let _ = self.write(|c| {
            c.execute_batch("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)")?;
            Ok(())
        });
    }

    /// Drops the sender so the thread drains and exits, then joins it.
    pub fn close(&self) {
        self.tx.lock().unwrap().take();
        if let Some(t) = self.thread.lock().unwrap().take() {
            let _ = t.join();
        }
    }
}
```

`core/src/store/settings.rs`:

```rust
use rusqlite::{params, Connection, OptionalExtension};
use serde::{de::DeserializeOwned, Serialize};

use crate::contract::CoreError;

pub const PREFERENCES: &str = "preferences";
pub const SUBTITLE_DEFAULTS: &str = "subtitle_defaults";
pub const AUTO_SKIP: &str = "auto_skip";
pub const MAIN_TRACKER: &str = "main_tracker";
pub const AUTO_MATCH_VERSION: &str = "auto_match_version";
pub const WATCHING_FETCHED_AT: &str = "watching_fetched_at";

pub fn get<T: DeserializeOwned>(conn: &Connection, key: &str) -> Result<Option<T>, CoreError> {
    let raw: Option<String> = conn
        .query_row("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?1", params![key], |r| r.get(0))
        .optional()?;
    match raw {
        Some(s) => Ok(Some(serde_json::from_str(&s)?)),
        None => Ok(None),
    }
}

pub fn set<T: Serialize>(conn: &Connection, key: &str, value: &T) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
    let json = serde_json::to_string(value)?;
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?1, ?2) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value",
        params![key, json],
    )?;
    Ok(())
}

pub fn get_or_default<T: DeserializeOwned + Default>(conn: &Connection, key: &str) -> Result<T, CoreError> {
    Ok(get(conn, key)?.unwrap_or_default())
}
```

### Task 4: The event bus and the events ring

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/events.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Store`, `Event`, `EventBody`, `EventListener`, `Level`, `Stage`, `JobRef`, `time::*`.
- Produces:
  - `EventBus::new(store: Arc<Store>) -> Result<Arc<EventBus>, CoreError>`: reads the ring's highest `seq` so numbering continues across launches.
  - `EventBus::emit(&self, level: Level, stage: Stage, message: impl Into<String>, job: Option<JobRef>, body: EventBody) -> Event`: stamps `seq` and `at`, persists Info and above to `events` trimmed to the last 2000, then calls every listener.
  - `EventBus::info/warn/error/debug(&self, stage, message, body)` shorthands with `job: None`.
  - `EventBus::subscribe(&self, listener: Arc<dyn EventListener>) -> u64` and `unsubscribe(&self, id: u64)`.
  - `EventBus::recent(&self, limit: u64) -> Result<Vec<Event>, CoreError>` oldest first; `clear(&self) -> Result<(), CoreError>`.
  - `Subscription` object (uniffi `Object`) with `unsubscribe(&self)` and a `Drop` that unsubscribes.
  - `Collector`: a listener that records events into a `Mutex<Vec<Event>>`, plain `pub` and `#[doc(hidden)]`, since the CLI's `--wait` and every integration test use it.

```rust
        assert_eq!(all[0].message, "m10");
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the bus**

`core/src/events.rs`:

```rust
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, RwLock};

use rusqlite::params;

use crate::contract::*;
use crate::store::Store;
use crate::time;

pub const RING_SIZE: u64 = 2000;

pub struct EventBus {
    seq: AtomicU64,
    next_listener: AtomicU64,
    listeners: RwLock<Vec<(u64, Arc<dyn EventListener>)>>,
    store: Arc<Store>,
}

impl EventBus {
    pub fn new(store: Arc<Store>) -> Result<Arc<EventBus>, CoreError> {
        let last: i64 = store.write(|c| Ok(c.query_row("SELECT coalesce(max(seq), 0) FROM events", [], |r| r.get(0))?))?;
        Ok(Arc::new(EventBus {
            seq: AtomicU64::new(last as u64),
            next_listener: AtomicU64::new(1),
            listeners: RwLock::new(Vec::new()),
            store,
        }))
    }

    pub fn emit(&self, level: Level, stage: Stage, message: impl Into<String>, job: Option<JobRef>, body: EventBody) -> Event {
        let event = Event {
            seq: self.seq.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1,
            at: time::now(),
            level,
            stage,
            message: message.into(),
            job,
            body,
        };
        if level != Level::Debug {
            self.persist(&event);
        }
        let listeners: Vec<Arc<dyn EventListener>> = self.listeners.read().unwrap().iter().map(|(_, l)| l.clone()).collect();
        for l in listeners {
            l.on_event(event.clone());
        }
        event
    }

    pub fn debug(&self, stage: Stage, message: impl Into<String>, body: EventBody) -> Event { self.emit(Level::Debug, stage, message, None, body) }
    pub fn info(&self, stage: Stage, message: impl Into<String>, body: EventBody) -> Event { self.emit(Level::Info, stage, message, None, body) }
    pub fn warn(&self, stage: Stage, message: impl Into<String>, body: EventBody) -> Event { self.emit(Level::Warn, stage, message, None, body) }
    pub fn error(&self, stage: Stage, message: impl Into<String>, body: EventBody) -> Event { self.emit(Level::Error, stage, message, None, body) }

    fn persist(&self, event: &Event) {
        let e = event.clone();
        let _ = self.store.write(move |c| {
            let (job_id, job_kind, job_phase) = match &e.job {
                Some(j) => (Some(j.id as i64), Some(j.kind.as_str()), Some(j.phase.as_str())),
                None => (None, None, None),
            };
            c.execute(
                "INSERT INTO events (seq, at, level, stage, message, job_id, job_kind, job_phase, body) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8, ?9)",
                params![e.seq as i64, time::to_secs(e.at), e.level.as_str(), e.stage.as_str(), e.message, job_id, job_kind, job_phase, serde_json::to_string(&e.body)?],
            )?;
            c.execute(
                "DELETE FROM events WHERE seq <= (SELECT seq FROM events ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET ?1)",
                params![RING_SIZE as i64],
            )?;
            Ok(())
        });
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self, listener: Arc<dyn EventListener>) -> u64 {
        let id = self.next_listener.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        self.listeners.write().unwrap().push((id, listener));
        id
    }

    pub fn unsubscribe(&self, id: u64) {
        self.listeners.write().unwrap().retain(|(i, _)| *i != id);
    }

    pub fn recent(&self, limit: u64) -> Result<Vec<Event>, CoreError> {
        self.store.write(move |c| {
            let mut stmt = c.prepare_cached(
                "SELECT seq, at, level, stage, message, job_id, job_kind, job_phase, body FROM events ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT ?1",
            )?;
            let mut rows: Vec<Event> = stmt
                .query_map(params![limit as i64], |r| {
                    let job = match (r.get::<_, Option<i64>>(5)?, r.get::<_, Option<String>>(6)?, r.get::<_, Option<String>>(7)?) {
                        (Some(id), Some(kind), Some(phase)) => Some(JobRef {
                            id: id as u64,
                            kind: JobKind::from_str(&kind).unwrap_or(JobKind::Scan),
                            phase: JobPhase::from_str(&phase).unwrap_or(JobPhase::Finished),
                        }),
                        _ => None,
                    };
                    let body: String = r.get(8)?;
                    Ok(Event {
                        seq: r.get::<_, i64>(0)? as u64,
                        at: time::from_secs(r.get(1)?),
                        level: Level::from_str(&r.get::<_, String>(2)?).unwrap_or(Level::Info),
                        stage: Stage::from_str(&r.get::<_, String>(3)?).unwrap_or(Stage::System),
                        message: r.get(4)?,
                        job,
                        body: serde_json::from_str(&body).unwrap_or(EventBody::Notice),
                    })
                })?
                .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
            rows.reverse();
            Ok(rows)
        })
    }

    pub fn clear(&self) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        self.store.write(|c| { c.execute("DELETE FROM events", [])?; Ok(()) })
    }
}

/// Handed back by `Core::subscribe`; dropping it or calling `unsubscribe`
/// removes the listener.
#[derive(uniffi::Object)]
pub struct Subscription {
    bus: Arc<EventBus>,
    id: u64,
    active: AtomicBool,
}

impl Subscription {
    pub fn new(bus: Arc<EventBus>, listener: Arc<dyn EventListener>) -> Arc<Subscription> {
        let id = bus.subscribe(listener);
        Arc::new(Subscription { bus, id, active: AtomicBool::new(true) })
    }
}

#[uniffi::export]
impl Subscription {
    pub fn unsubscribe(&self) {
        if self.active.swap(false, Ordering::SeqCst) {
            self.bus.unsubscribe(self.id);
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for Subscription {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.unsubscribe();
    }
}

/// A listener that keeps everything it saw. Tests and the CLI use it.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Collector {
    events: Mutex<Vec<Event>>,
}

impl Collector {
    pub fn events(&self) -> Vec<Event> {
        self.events.lock().unwrap().clone()
    }
    pub fn bodies(&self) -> Vec<EventBody> {
        self.events().into_iter().map(|e| e.body).collect()
    }
}

impl EventListener for Collector {
    fn on_event(&self, event: Event) {
        self.events.lock().unwrap().push(event);
    }
}
```

`JobKind::from_str`, `JobPhase::from_str`, `Level::from_str`, `Stage::from_str` are Task 2's; `JobPhase::as_str` and `JobKind::as_str` too.

### Task 5: Jobs: the registry, cancellation, one at a time per kind, the progress throttle

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/jobs.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EventBus`, `JobKind`, `JobRef`, `JobPhase`, `JobInfo`, `Progress`, `EventBody`, `CoreError`.
- Produces:
  - `Jobs::new(runtime: tokio::runtime::Handle, bus: Arc<EventBus>) -> Arc<Jobs>`.
  - `Jobs::start<F, Fut>(&self, kind: JobKind, f: F) -> u64` where `F: FnOnce(Arc<JobCtx>) -> Fut + Send + 'static`, `Fut: Future<Output = Result<Finished, CoreError>> + Send + 'static`. Emits `JobStarted` (Debug, phase Started), runs the future on the runtime, emits the terminal event with phase Finished: the `Finished` the job returned, or `JobFailed { error }` at Error, or `JobCancelled` at Info when the token was cancelled. For a kind with `one_at_a_time()` and a running job of that kind, returns the running id and starts nothing.
  - `Jobs::cancel(&self, id: u64) -> Result<(), CoreError>`: `NotFound { what: Job, id }` for an unknown or finished job.
  - `Jobs::cancel_all(&self)` for shutdown.
  - `Jobs::list(&self) -> Vec<JobInfo>`.
  - `Jobs::running(&self, kind: JobKind) -> Option<u64>`.
  - `JobCtx { id, kind, cancel: CancellationToken, .. }` with `progress(&self, done: u64, total: Option<u64>, label: &str)` (throttled to four a second, the latest value always flushed), `emit(&self, level, message, body)` (tagged with phase Running), `is_cancelled(&self) -> bool`, `checkpoint(&self) -> Result<(), CoreError>` (returns `Err(Internal { message: "cancelled" })` when cancelled, so a job's `?` unwinds; the runner turns that into `JobCancelled`).
  - `Finished { level: Level, message: String, body: EventBody }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::contract::*;
    use crate::events::{Collector, EventBus};
    use crate::store::Store;
    use std::time::Duration;

    fn setup() -> (tempfile::TempDir, tokio::runtime::Runtime, Arc<Jobs>, Arc<Collector>) {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let store = Store::open(&dir.path().join("anibeam.db")).unwrap();
        let bus = EventBus::new(store).unwrap();
        let collector = Arc::new(Collector::default());
        bus.subscribe(collector.clone());
        let rt = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_multi_thread().worker_threads(2).enable_all().build().unwrap();
        let jobs = Jobs::new(rt.handle().clone(), bus);
        (dir, rt, jobs, collector)
    }

    fn wait_for_finished(c: &Collector, id: u64) {
        for _ in 0..200 {
            if c.events().iter().any(|e| e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == id && j.phase == JobPhase::Finished)) {
                return;
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
        }
        panic!("job {id} never finished");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_job_starts_runs_and_finishes_with_its_own_terminal_event() {
        let (_d, _rt, jobs, c) = setup();
        let id = jobs.start(JobKind::Search, |ctx| async move {
            ctx.emit(Level::Debug, "half way", EventBody::Notice);
            Ok(Finished { level: Level::Debug, message: "search done".into(), body: EventBody::SearchFinished { results: vec![] } })
        });
        wait_for_finished(&c, id);
        let mine: Vec<Event> = c.events().into_iter().filter(|e| e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == id)).collect();
        assert_eq!(mine[0].job.as_ref().unwrap().phase, JobPhase::Started);
        assert!(matches!(mine[0].body, EventBody::JobStarted { kind: JobKind::Search }));
        assert_eq!(mine[1].job.as_ref().unwrap().phase, JobPhase::Running);
        assert!(matches!(mine.last().unwrap().body, EventBody::SearchFinished { .. }));
        assert!(jobs.list().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failing_job_ends_in_job_failed() {
        let (_d, _rt, jobs, c) = setup();
        let id = jobs.start(JobKind::Search, |_ctx| async move { Err(CoreError::internal("nope")) });
        wait_for_finished(&c, id);
        let last = c.events().into_iter().filter(|e| e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == id)).last().unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(last.body, EventBody::JobFailed { .. }));
        assert_eq!(last.level, Level::Error);
    }

    #[test]
    fn cancel_ends_a_job_with_job_cancelled() {
        let (_d, _rt, jobs, c) = setup();
        let id = jobs.start(JobKind::Scan, |ctx| async move {
            loop {
                ctx.checkpoint()?;
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5)).await;
            }
        });
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(30));
        jobs.cancel(id).unwrap();
        wait_for_finished(&c, id);
        let last = c.events().into_iter().filter(|e| e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == id)).last().unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(last.body, EventBody::JobCancelled));
        assert!(matches!(jobs.cancel(id), Err(CoreError::NotFound { what: Entity::Job, .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn one_at_a_time_kinds_return_the_running_id() {
        let (_d, _rt, jobs, c) = setup();
        let first = jobs.start(JobKind::Scan, |ctx| async move {
            while !ctx.is_cancelled() { tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5)).await; }
            Ok(Finished { level: Level::Info, message: "".into(), body: EventBody::ScanFinished { source: None, added: 0, changed: 0, removed: 0 } })
        });
        let second = jobs.start(JobKind::Scan, |_ctx| async move { panic!("must not run") });
        assert_eq!(first, second);
        let other = jobs.start(JobKind::Search, |_ctx| async move { Ok(Finished { level: Level::Debug, message: "".into(), body: EventBody::SearchFinished { results: vec![] } }) });
        assert_ne!(other, first);
        jobs.cancel(first).unwrap();
        wait_for_finished(&c, first);
    }

    #[test]
    fn progress_is_throttled_to_four_a_second_and_the_last_value_lands() {
        let (_d, _rt, jobs, c) = setup();
        let id = jobs.start(JobKind::Scan, |ctx| async move {
            for i in 0..100u64 { ctx.progress(i, Some(100), "walking"); }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
            Ok(Finished { level: Level::Info, message: "".into(), body: EventBody::ScanFinished { source: None, added: 0, changed: 0, removed: 0 } })
        });
        wait_for_finished(&c, id);
        let progress: Vec<u64> = c.events().into_iter().filter_map(|e| match e.body { EventBody::JobProgress { done, .. } if e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == id) => Some(done), _ => None }).collect();
        assert!(progress.len() <= 3, "{progress:?}");
        assert_eq!(*progress.last().unwrap(), 99);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the registry**

`core/src/jobs.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::future::Future;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime};

use tokio::runtime::Handle;
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;

use crate::contract::*;
use crate::events::EventBus;
use crate::time;

pub const PROGRESS_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(250);

pub struct Finished {
    pub level: Level,
    pub message: String,
    pub body: EventBody,
}

struct Running {
    kind: JobKind,
    started_at: SystemTime,
    cancel: CancellationToken,
    progress: Arc<Mutex<Option<Progress>>>,
}

pub struct Jobs {
    runtime: Handle,
    next_id: AtomicU64,
    running: Mutex<HashMap<u64, Running>>,
    bus: Arc<EventBus>,
}

pub struct JobCtx {
    pub id: u64,
    pub kind: JobKind,
    pub cancel: CancellationToken,
    bus: Arc<EventBus>,
    runtime: Handle,
    progress: Arc<Mutex<Option<Progress>>>,
    throttle: Mutex<Throttle>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct Throttle {
    last_emit: Option<Instant>,
    pending: Option<Progress>,
    flush_scheduled: bool,
}

impl JobCtx {
    fn job_ref(&self, phase: JobPhase) -> JobRef {
        JobRef { id: self.id, kind: self.kind, phase }
    }

    pub fn emit(&self, level: Level, message: impl Into<String>, body: EventBody) {
        self.bus.emit(level, self.kind.stage(), message, Some(self.job_ref(JobPhase::Running)), body);
    }

    pub fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool {
        self.cancel.is_cancelled()
    }

    /// `?` on this at the top of every loop body; the runner turns the error
    /// into JobCancelled.
    pub fn checkpoint(&self) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        if self.is_cancelled() { Err(CoreError::internal("cancelled")) } else { Ok(()) }
    }

    /// At most four JobProgress events a second per job. A burst of calls
    /// keeps only the latest value, flushed 250 ms after the last emit.
    pub fn progress(self: &Arc<Self>, done: u64, total: Option<u64>, label: &str) {
        let p = Progress { done, total, label: label.to_string() };
        *self.progress.lock().unwrap() = Some(p.clone());
        let mut t = self.throttle.lock().unwrap();
        let due = t.last_emit.map_or(true, |l| l.elapsed() >= PROGRESS_INTERVAL);
        if due {
            t.last_emit = Some(Instant::now());
            t.pending = None;
            drop(t);
            self.emit_progress(p);
        } else {
            t.pending = Some(p);
            if !t.flush_scheduled {
                t.flush_scheduled = true;
                let wait = PROGRESS_INTERVAL.saturating_sub(t.last_emit.unwrap().elapsed());
                let me = self.clone();
                self.runtime.spawn(async move {
                    tokio::time::sleep(wait).await;
                    let pending = {
                        let mut t = me.throttle.lock().unwrap();
                        t.flush_scheduled = false;
                        t.last_emit = Some(Instant::now());
                        t.pending.take()
                    };
                    if let Some(p) = pending { me.emit_progress(p); }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    fn emit_progress(&self, p: Progress) {
        self.emit(Level::Debug, format!("{} {}", self.kind.as_str(), p.label), EventBody::JobProgress { done: p.done, total: p.total, label: p.label });
    }
}

impl Jobs {
    pub fn new(runtime: Handle, bus: Arc<EventBus>) -> Arc<Jobs> {
        Arc::new(Jobs { runtime, next_id: AtomicU64::new(1), running: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()), bus })
    }

    pub fn running(&self, kind: JobKind) -> Option<u64> {
        self.running.lock().unwrap().iter().find(|(_, r)| r.kind == kind).map(|(id, _)| *id)
    }

    pub fn start<F, Fut>(self: &Arc<Self>, kind: JobKind, f: F) -> u64
    where
        F: FnOnce(Arc<JobCtx>) -> Fut + Send + 'static,
        Fut: Future<Output = Result<Finished, CoreError>> + Send + 'static,
    {
        let mut running = self.running.lock().unwrap();
        if kind.one_at_a_time() {
            if let Some((id, _)) = running.iter().find(|(_, r)| r.kind == kind) {
                return *id;
            }
        }
        let id = self.next_id.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        let cancel = CancellationToken::new();
        let progress = Arc::new(Mutex::new(None));
        running.insert(id, Running { kind, started_at: time::now(), cancel: cancel.clone(), progress: progress.clone() });
        drop(running);

        let ctx = Arc::new(JobCtx { id, kind, cancel: cancel.clone(), bus: self.bus.clone(), runtime: self.runtime.clone(), progress, throttle: Mutex::new(Throttle::default()) });
        self.bus.emit(Level::Debug, kind.stage(), format!("{} started", kind.as_str()), Some(JobRef { id, kind, phase: JobPhase::Started }), EventBody::JobStarted { kind });

        let jobs = self.clone();
        self.runtime.spawn(async move {
            let outcome = tokio::select! {
                _ = cancel.cancelled() => None,
                r = f(ctx.clone()) => Some(r),
            };
            jobs.running.lock().unwrap().remove(&id);
            let finished = JobRef { id, kind, phase: JobPhase::Finished };
            match outcome {
                None => { jobs.bus.emit(Level::Info, kind.stage(), format!("{} cancelled", kind.as_str()), Some(finished), EventBody::JobCancelled); }
                Some(Err(e)) if e == CoreError::internal("cancelled") => { jobs.bus.emit(Level::Info, kind.stage(), format!("{} cancelled", kind.as_str()), Some(finished), EventBody::JobCancelled); }
                Some(Err(e)) => { jobs.bus.emit(Level::Error, kind.stage(), format!("{} failed: {e}", kind.as_str()), Some(finished), EventBody::JobFailed { error: e }); }
                Some(Ok(done)) => { jobs.bus.emit(done.level, kind.stage(), done.message, Some(finished), done.body); }
            }
        });
        id
    }

    pub fn cancel(&self, id: u64) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        match self.running.lock().unwrap().get(&id) {
            Some(r) => { r.cancel.cancel(); Ok(()) }
            None => Err(CoreError::NotFound { what: Entity::Job, id }),
        }
    }

    pub fn cancel_all(&self) {
        for r in self.running.lock().unwrap().values() { r.cancel.cancel(); }
    }

    pub fn list(&self) -> Vec<JobInfo> {
        let mut out: Vec<JobInfo> = self.running.lock().unwrap().iter()
            .map(|(id, r)| JobInfo { id: *id, kind: r.kind, started_at: r.started_at, progress: r.progress.lock().unwrap().clone() })
            .collect();
        out.sort_by_key(|j| j.id);
        out
    }
}
```

Note `tokio::select!` drops the job's future on cancellation, which is what ends a job stuck in `await`; the `checkpoint` path covers a job in a tight CPU loop. A job that must clean up on cancellation (the OAuth listener, the anirss child) wraps its body so the drop of its guards does the cleanup.

### Task 6: The core object, the dispatch, and the CLI door

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/core.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/lib.rs`
- Create: `core/tests/common/mod.rs`
- Create: `core/tests/core_open.rs`
- Replace: `apps/cli/src/main.rs`
- Modify: `apps/cli/Cargo.toml` (add `serde_json.workspace = true`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces:
  - `Core::open(paths: CorePaths) -> Result<Arc<Core>, CoreError>`: opens and migrates the database, builds the runtime (multi-thread, four workers named `anibeam-core`), the bus and the jobs, nothing else.
  - `Core::start(&self) -> Result<(), CoreError>`: Task 31 fills it; here it emits `Ready` and sets a `started` flag; a second call is `Ok` and does nothing.
  - `Core::call(&self, call: Call) -> Result<Reply, CoreError>`: the dispatch. This task implements `About`, `RecentEvents`, `ClearEvents`, `ListJobs`, `CancelJob`; every other arm returns `Err(Unsupported { what: "<CallName> is not built yet" })` and later tasks replace their arms. The last task deletes the catch-all.
  - `Core::subscribe(&self, listener: Arc<dyn EventListener>) -> Arc<Subscription>`.
  - `Core::shutdown(&self)`: cancels jobs, stops the watcher (Task 10 adds it), checkpoints, closes the store; idempotent.
  - `Core` fields visible in the crate: `paths`, `store`, `bus`, `jobs`, `runtime: tokio::runtime::Runtime`, `http: Arc<dyn Http>` (Task 13 adds it; leave a comment), plus later modules' state.
  - `core/tests/common/mod.rs`: `open_core() -> (TempDir, Arc<Core>, Arc<Collector>)` and `wait_for(collector, |event| bool, timeout)` helpers every integration test uses. `wait_job(collector, job_id) -> Event` resolves on the first event whose `job.phase` is `Finished`, the "await job N" helper the contract asks both shells to write.
  - The CLI: `anibeam-cli [--root DIR] call <Name> [--json '{...}'] [--wait]` and `anibeam-cli events [--follow] [--level info]`. `--root` puts every directory under one path (`CorePaths::under`), for tests and for pointing at a sandbox; without it the XDG paths apply. The CLI never calls `start`.

`core/tests/common/mod.rs`:

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use anibeam_core::events::Collector;
use anibeam_core::{Core, CorePaths, Event, JobPhase};

pub fn open_core() -> (tempfile::TempDir, Arc<Core>, Arc<Collector>) {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let core = Core::open(CorePaths::under(dir.path())).unwrap();
    let collector = Arc::new(Collector::default());
    let sub = core.subscribe(collector.clone());
    std::mem::forget(sub);
    (dir, core, collector)
}

pub fn wait_for(c: &Collector, pred: impl Fn(&Event) -> bool, timeout: Duration) -> Event {
    let start = Instant::now();
    loop {
        if let Some(e) = c.events().into_iter().find(|e| pred(e)) {
            return e;
        }
        assert!(start.elapsed() < timeout, "timed out waiting; saw {:#?}", c.events());
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
    }
}

/// The contract's "await job N": the first event of that job whose phase is Finished.
pub fn wait_job(c: &Collector, job: u64) -> Event {
    wait_for(c, |e| e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == job && j.phase == JobPhase::Finished), Duration::from_secs(30))
}
```

```rust
mod common;

#[test]
fn open_about_and_events_work_without_start() {
    let (dir, core, c) = common::open_core();
    match core.call(Call::About).unwrap() {
        Reply::About { about } => {
            assert_eq!(about.version, VERSION);
            assert!(about.db_path.starts_with(dir.path().to_str().unwrap()));
        }
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    core.start().unwrap();
    common::wait_for(&c, |e| matches!(e.body, EventBody::Ready), std::time::Duration::from_secs(2));
    match core.call(Call::RecentEvents { limit: 10 }).unwrap() {
        Reply::Events { events } => assert!(events.iter().any(|e| matches!(e.body, EventBody::Ready))),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),

    assert!(matches!(core.call(Call::ClearEvents), Ok(Reply::Ok)));
    assert!(matches!(core.call(Call::ListJobs), Ok(Reply::Jobs { .. })));
    assert!(matches!(core.call(Call::CancelJob { job: 999 }), Err(CoreError::NotFound { what: Entity::Job, id: 999 })));
    core.shutdown();
    core.shutdown();
}
```
