### Task 20: Tracker accounts, credentials and the OAuth flows

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/trackers/accounts.rs`
- Create: `core/src/trackers/oauth.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/core.rs` (arms for `GetTrackers`, `SetTrackerCredentials`, `ConnectTracker`, `DisconnectTracker`, `SetMainTracker`; `pub(crate) oauth_port: AtomicU16` default 53682 with a `#[doc(hidden)] pub fn set_oauth_port`)
- Create: `core/tests/oauth.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Secrets`, `AnilistClient::graphql` with `VIEWER_QUERY`, `MalClient`, `prefs::{load_main_tracker, save_main_tracker}`, the `tracker_accounts` table.
- Produces:
  - `accounts::bundled_client_id(t: Tracker) -> Option<&'static str>` from `option_env!("ANIBEAM_ANILIST_CLIENT_ID")` and `option_env!("ANIBEAM_MAL_CLIENT_ID")`; `accounts::bundled_client_secret(t) -> Option<&'static str>` from `option_env!("ANIBEAM_MAL_CLIENT_SECRET")` (AniList needs none). Set at build time, inlined like Electron's Vite env.
  - `accounts::Row { user_id, username, client_id, expires_at, connected_at, synced_at, progress_fetched_at, secret_store }` and `accounts::load_row(conn, t) -> Result<Option<Row>, CoreError>`.
  - `accounts::state(core) -> Result<TrackerState, CoreError>`: per tracker, `connected = connected_at IS NOT NULL`, `client_id` = the row's, else the bundled id, else empty; `has_client_secret` = the keyring holds `<t>.client_secret` or a bundled secret exists; `bundled_credentials` = bundled id (AniList) or bundled id and secret (MAL); `last_sync = synced_at`; `main` from `prefs`.
  - `accounts::effective_credentials(core, t) -> Result<(String, Option<String>), CoreError>`: the row's client id else bundled, the keyring secret else bundled; `Invalid { field: "client_id" }` when neither exists; for MAL, `Invalid { field: "client_secret" }` when no secret exists.
  - `accounts::set_credentials(core, t, client_id, client_secret)`: upsert the row's `client_id` (a row may exist unconnected); a `Some` non-empty secret goes to the keyring; an empty or None secret keeps the stored one; emits `TrackersChanged`.
  - `accounts::disconnect(core, t)`: `UPDATE tracker_accounts SET user_id = NULL, username = NULL, expires_at = NULL, connected_at = NULL, synced_at = NULL, progress_fetched_at = NULL WHERE tracker = ?1`, `DELETE FROM tracker_entries WHERE tracker = ?1`, delete `<t>.access_token` and `<t>.refresh_token`; emits `TrackersChanged` at Debug and `SeriesChanged` at Debug with every card whose match carries that tracker's id (the numbers left). `client_id` and the client secret survive, this plan's decision.
  - `accounts::set_main(core, t)`: `prefs::save_main_tracker`, `TrackersChanged`, `SeriesChanged` for every matched series (the numbers may change source).
  - `accounts::access_token(core, t) -> Result<Option<String>, CoreError>`: the keyring value under the row's `secret_store` hint; for MAL, when `expires_at` is within one hour or past and a refresh token exists, refresh first: POST `https://myanimelist.net/v1/oauth2/token` with `client_id`, `client_secret`, `grant_type=refresh_token`, `refresh_token`; store the new tokens and `expires_at`; on failure emit Warn `MAL auth expired, reconnect in Settings.` and return None.
  - `oauth::connect(core, t) -> Result<u64, CoreError>`: cancels a running ConnectTracker job (a new connect tears the old one down), then starts a ConnectTracker job whose first act is to bind `127.0.0.1:<oauth_port>`; a bind failure ends the job in `JobFailed { Io { message: "port 53682 is in use, another AniBeam is mid-connect" } }`. Then: generate `state` (16 random bytes, base64url, no padding) and for MAL the verifier (32 random bytes, base64url); build `open_url` (AniList: `https://anilist.co/api/v2/oauth/authorize?client_id=<id>&response_type=token` and nothing else; MAL: `https://myanimelist.net/v1/oauth2/authorize?response_type=code&client_id=<id>&redirect_uri=<encoded>&state=<state>&code_challenge=<verifier>&code_challenge_method=plain`); emit `AuthUrlReady { tracker, open_url, redirect_url: "http://127.0.0.1:53682/callback" }` at Info; serve the listener under a five minute `tokio::time::timeout` (a timeout fails the job with `Authorization timed out, try again.`). One connection at a time; read up to 8 KB until `\r\n\r\n`; parse the request line; anything but `/callback` gets `404 not found`; a provider `error` query parameter fails the job with that error after serving the error page. AniList: a hit with neither `access_token` nor `error` gets the fragment forwarder page; a hit with `access_token` gets the success page, then `Viewer { id name }` with the bearer token validates it (a failure fails the job), `expires_at = now + expires_in` when present. MAL: `state` must equal the one sent, else the error page `state mismatch, possible CSRF, aborted.` and a failed job; a `code` gets the success page first, then the exchange POST (form `client_id`, `client_secret`, `code`, `code_verifier`, `grant_type=authorization_code`, `redirect_uri`) and `GET https://api.myanimelist.net/v2/users/@me`. On success: tokens to the keyring (`secret_store` set from the returned kind), the row upserted (`user_id`, `username`, `client_id`, `expires_at`, `connected_at = now`), events `TrackerConnected { tracker, username }` at Info and `TrackersChanged` at Debug; the terminal is `TrackerConnected`. Task 21 adds the forced progress refresh after it.
  - The three pages as constants `SUCCESS_PAGE(provider_label)`, `ERROR_PAGE(message)`, `FRAGMENT_FORWARDER`, copied from `src/main/handlers/trackerHandler.ts:61-83` with the titles `AniBeam, connected`, `AniBeam, auth failed` and `Connecting`. The HTTP reply is `HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8\r\nContent-Length: N\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n<body>`.

Carried from `src/main/handlers/trackerHandler.ts` (`buildAuthUrl`, `pkceVerifier`, `pkceChallenge`, `randomToken`, `bindLoopback`, the callback handler, `exchangeMalCode`, `fetchProfile`, `FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS`) and `src/shared/trackerConstants.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`core/tests/oauth.rs`, driving the listener from the test with `std::net::TcpStream`:

```rust
mod common;
use anibeam_core::*;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::TcpStream;
use std::time::Duration;

fn http_get(port: u16, path: &str) -> String {
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).unwrap();
    write!(s, "GET {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
    let mut out = String::new();
    s.read_to_string(&mut out).unwrap();
    out
}

#[test]
fn anilist_implicit_grant_through_the_forwarder_connects() {
    let http = anibeam_core::net::FakeHttp::new();
    let (_dir, core, c) = common::open_core_with_http(http.clone());
    core.set_oauth_port(53690);
    core.call(Call::SetTrackerCredentials { tracker: Tracker::Anilist, client_id: "123".into(), client_secret: None }).unwrap();
    http.push_json(200, serde_json::json!({ "data": { "Viewer": { "id": 42, "name": "bandit" } } }));
    let job = match core.call(Call::ConnectTracker { tracker: Tracker::Anilist }).unwrap() { Reply::Started { job } => job, other => panic!("{other:?}") };
    let ready = common::wait_for(&c, |e| matches!(e.body, EventBody::AuthUrlReady { .. }), Duration::from_secs(5));
    match ready.body { EventBody::AuthUrlReady { open_url, redirect_url, .. } => {
        assert_eq!(open_url, "https://anilist.co/api/v2/oauth/authorize?client_id=123&response_type=token");
        assert_eq!(redirect_url, "http://127.0.0.1:53690/callback");
    } _ => unreachable!() }
    assert!(http_get(53690, "/nope").starts_with("HTTP/1.1 404"));
    let first = http_get(53690, "/callback");
    assert!(first.contains("window.location.hash"));
    let second = http_get(53690, "/callback?access_token=tok&expires_in=3600&token_type=Bearer");
    assert!(second.contains("AniList connected"));
    let done = common::wait_job(&c, job);
    assert!(matches!(done.body, EventBody::TrackerConnected { tracker: Tracker::Anilist, ref username } if username == "bandit"), "{done:?}");
    match core.call(Call::GetTrackers).unwrap() {
        Reply::Trackers { state } => {
            assert!(state.anilist.connected);
            assert_eq!(state.anilist.username.as_deref(), Some("bandit"));
            assert_eq!(state.anilist.client_id, "123");
            assert!(state.anilist.expires_at.is_some());
        }
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    let viewer = &http.requests()[0];
    assert!(viewer.headers.iter().any(|(k, v)| k == "Authorization" && v == "Bearer tok"));

    assert!(matches!(core.call(Call::DisconnectTracker { tracker: Tracker::Anilist }).unwrap(), Reply::Ok));
    match core.call(Call::GetTrackers).unwrap() {
        Reply::Trackers { state } => { assert!(!state.anilist.connected); assert_eq!(state.anilist.client_id, "123"); }
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
}

#[test]
fn mal_pkce_flow_checks_state_and_exchanges_the_code() {
    let http = anibeam_core::net::FakeHttp::new();
    let (_dir, core, c) = common::open_core_with_http(http.clone());
    core.set_oauth_port(53691);
    core.call(Call::SetTrackerCredentials { tracker: Tracker::Mal, client_id: "mid".into(), client_secret: Some("sec".into()) }).unwrap();
    http.push_for("oauth2/token", 200, serde_json::json!({ "access_token": "mtok", "refresh_token": "mref", "expires_in": 2415600 }).to_string());
    http.push_for("users/@me", 200, serde_json::json!({ "id": 7, "name": "bandit" }).to_string());
    let job = match core.call(Call::ConnectTracker { tracker: Tracker::Mal }).unwrap() { Reply::Started { job } => job, other => panic!("{other:?}") };
    let ready = common::wait_for(&c, |e| matches!(e.body, EventBody::AuthUrlReady { .. }), Duration::from_secs(5));
    let open_url = match ready.body { EventBody::AuthUrlReady { open_url, .. } => open_url, _ => unreachable!() };
    assert!(open_url.starts_with("https://myanimelist.net/v1/oauth2/authorize?response_type=code&client_id=mid&redirect_uri=http%3A%2F%2F127.0.0.1%3A53691%2Fcallback&state="));
    assert!(open_url.contains("&code_challenge_method=plain"));
    let state = open_url.split("state=").nth(1).unwrap().split('&').next().unwrap().to_string();
    let bad = http_get(53691, "/callback?code=abc&state=wrong");
    assert!(bad.contains("state mismatch"));
    let failed = common::wait_job(&c, job);
    assert!(matches!(failed.body, EventBody::JobFailed { .. }));

    let job = match core.call(Call::ConnectTracker { tracker: Tracker::Mal }).unwrap() { Reply::Started { job } => job, other => panic!() };
    let ready = common::wait_for(&c, |e| matches!(e.body, EventBody::AuthUrlReady { .. }) && e.job.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| j.id == job), Duration::from_secs(5));
    let open_url = match ready.body { EventBody::AuthUrlReady { open_url, .. } => open_url, _ => unreachable!() };
    let state = open_url.split("state=").nth(1).unwrap().split('&').next().unwrap().to_string();
    let verifier = open_url.split("code_challenge=").nth(1).unwrap().split('&').next().unwrap().to_string();
    let ok = http_get(53691, &format!("/callback?code=abc&state={state}"));
    assert!(ok.contains("MyAnimeList connected"));
    let done = common::wait_job(&c, job);
    assert!(matches!(done.body, EventBody::TrackerConnected { tracker: Tracker::Mal, .. }), "{done:?}");
    let exchange = http.requests().into_iter().find(|r| r.url.contains("oauth2/token")).unwrap();
    match exchange.body { Some(anibeam_core::net::Body::Form(f)) => {
        assert!(f.contains(&("code_verifier".into(), verifier)));
        assert!(f.contains(&("grant_type".into(), "authorization_code".into())));
        assert!(f.contains(&("client_secret".into(), "sec".into())));
    } _ => panic!() }
    let _ = state;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the modules**

`oauth.rs` uses `tokio::net::TcpListener::from_std` on a `std::net::TcpListener` bound with `SO_REUSEADDR` (`socket2` is not a dependency; bind through std, set nonblocking, convert) and `tokio::io::AsyncReadExt/AsyncWriteExt`; query parameters are parsed by splitting on `&` and `=` and percent-decoding (`+` as space, `%XX` bytes). Random bytes come from `rand::rng().fill_bytes`; base64url without padding from `base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD`.

### Task 21: The tracker cache and the five minute rule

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/trackers/cache.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/trackers/oauth.rs` (the post-connect refresh), `core/src/core.rs` (`RefreshProgress` arm)
- Create: `core/tests/tracker_cache.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `accounts::access_token`, `AnilistClient::graphql` with `MEDIA_LIST_COLLECTION_QUERY`, `MalClient::get`, `cards::cards_for`.
- Produces:
  - `cache::normalize_status(t: Tracker, raw: Option<&str>, is_rewatching: bool) -> Option<ListStatus>`: AniList `CURRENT`, `PLANNING`, `COMPLETED`, `PAUSED`, `DROPPED`, `REPEATING` one to one; MAL `watching`, `plan_to_watch`, `completed`, `on_hold`, `dropped`, with `watching` plus `is_rewatching` giving Repeating; anything else None.
  - `cache::Entry { media_id: u64, progress: u32, status: Option<ListStatus>, score: Option<f64>, repeat: u32, updated_at: Option<i64> }`.
  - `cache::fetch_anilist(core, user_id, token) -> Result<Vec<Entry>, CoreError>` (15 s timeout; `score(format: POINT_10_DECIMAL)`; a score of 0 is None; `repeat` 0 is 0).
  - `cache::fetch_mal(core, token) -> Result<Vec<Entry>, CoreError>`: `GET https://api.myanimelist.net/v2/users/@me/animelist?fields=list_status{status,num_episodes_watched,is_rewatching,num_times_rewatched,score}&limit=1000&offset=N`, paging while `paging.next` exists and the page was full, at most 50 pages, 15 s per page.
  - `cache::replace(tx, t, entries, now)`: delete that tracker's rows, insert the new ones with `fetched_at = now`, keep an existing row's `updated_at` when the new one has none, set `progress_fetched_at = now` on the account.
  - `cache::patch_progress(conn, t, media_id, progress, status: Option<ListStatus>, now)` (keeps score and repeat), `cache::patch_score(conn, t, media_id, score: Option<f64>, status: Option<ListStatus>, now)` (keeps progress and repeat): `setProgressEntry` and `setProgressScoreAndStatus`.
  - `cache::PROGRESS_FRESH: Duration = 5 minutes`.
  - `cache::start_refresh(core, tracker: Option<Tracker>, force: bool) -> u64`: the RefreshProgress job (one at a time): the requested tracker, or every connected one; a tracker with no `connected_at` is skipped; inside the window and not forced, skipped with a Debug line `<t> progress is fresh`; else fetched and replaced; a failure keeps the rows and emits a Warn line with the sanitised message (Task 22's `sanitize`; until then the raw message); each refreshed tracker emits `ProgressRefreshed { tracker }` at Info; the job ends with the last `ProgressRefreshed`, or `Notice` `nothing to refresh` at Debug when nothing ran, or `JobFailed` when every requested tracker failed; `SeriesChanged` for every matched series after a refresh.
  - The post-connect refresh: `oauth::connect` ends with `cache::start_refresh(core, Some(t), true)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the module**

As specified. The MAL page loop:

```rust
let mut offset = 0u64;
for page in 0..50 {
    let url = format!("https://api.myanimelist.net/v2/users/@me/animelist?fields=list_status{{status,num_episodes_watched,is_rewatching,num_times_rewatched,score}}&limit=1000&offset={offset}");
    let r = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(15), core.mal.get(&url, &token)).await.map_err(|_| CoreError::Provider { provider: Provider::Mal, status: None, message: format!("MAL animelist page {page} timed out after 15000ms"), retry_after: None })??;
    // parse data[].node.id and data[].list_status; break when paging.next is absent or the page held fewer than 1000
    offset += 1000;
}
```

### Task 22: Marks, progress, scores, the hidden guard and sanitised errors

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/trackers/writes.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/core.rs` (arms for `MarkEpisode`, `SetProgress`, `SetScore`)
- Create: `core/tests/tracker_writes.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `accounts::access_token`, `AnilistClient::graphql` with `MEDIA_LIST_ENTRY_QUERY`, `SAVE_PROGRESS_MUTATION`, `SAVE_SCORE_MUTATION`, `MalClient::{get, patch_form}`, `cache::{patch_progress, patch_score}`, `cards::cards_for`.
- Produces:
  - `writes::sanitize(t: Tracker, status: Option<u32>, graphql_message: Option<&str>, mal_message: Option<&str>, raw: &str) -> String`: 429 `<Label> rate limited, try again in a minute.`; 401 or 403 `<Label> auth expired, reconnect in Settings.`; 404 `<Label> entry not found.`; 500 and above `<Label> server error (<status>), try again later.`; else the first GraphQL message, else MAL's `message`, else the raw error, the first of those under 200 characters; else `<Label> error, see activity log.` `<Label>` is `AniList` or `MAL`.
  - `writes::is_hidden(conn, t: Tracker, media_id: u64) -> Result<bool, CoreError>`: any `series` row with `hidden = 1` whose `anilist_id` (AniList) or `mal_id` (MAL) equals the id; ids never cross.
  - `writes::Targets { series, anilist: Option<u64>, mal: Option<u64>, total: Option<u32>, folder_name }` and `writes::targets(conn, series) -> Result<Targets, CoreError>`.
  - `writes::mark(core, series, episode: f64) -> Result<u64, CoreError>`: `NotFound`; `Refused(Extra)` never applies here (the call names a series and a number); `Invalid { field: "episode" }` when the floored number is 0 or below; `Refused(NoMatch)` with no ids; `Refused(Hidden)` when hidden for any carried id; `NotConnected { main }` when no tracker with a carried id is connected; `Refused(NotNewer)` when the main tracker's cached row already covers the episode. The Mark job writes to every connected tracker whose id the match carries, one `TrackerOutcome` each: read the current entry (AniList `MediaList(userId, mediaId) { progress status }`, always with the user id; MAL `GET /anime/{id}?fields=my_list_status`, a 404 is progress 0); `ok: false, reason: NotNewer, progress: current` when the current progress is at or above the episode; else write completed when `total` is known and the episode reaches it, else watching (AniList `SaveMediaListEntry(mediaId, progress, status)` with `COMPLETED` or `CURRENT`; MAL `PATCH /anime/{id}/my_list_status` with `num_watched_episodes` and `status` `completed` or `watching`); then `synced_at = now`, `patch_progress`, the Info line `<t> <old> -> <new> (mediaId <id>)`; a failure is `ok: false, message: sanitize(...)` and an Error line with the full message. Terminal `Marked { series, episode, outcomes }` at Info, message `marked episode N of <folder>`, then `SeriesChanged`.
  - `writes::set_progress(core, series, progress: u32) -> Result<u64, CoreError>`: the same guards without `NotNewer`; the status is planning at 0 (`PLANNING` / `plan_to_watch`), completed at or past a known total, else watching; terminal `ProgressSet { series, progress, outcomes }`, line `<t> set <old> -> <new> (mediaId <id>)`.
  - `writes::set_score(core, series, score: Option<f64>) -> Result<u64, CoreError>`: `Invalid { field: "score" }` outside 0 to 10; None is 0; the same guards; AniList `SaveMediaListEntry(mediaId, scoreRaw: round(score * 10), status)` with `status` `COMPLETED` when the current progress is at or past a known total above 0, else omitted (send the mutation without the `status` variable); MAL `score = round(score)` and `status=completed` only in that case; `patch_score` with `None` for 0; terminal `Scored { series, score, outcomes }`, line `<t> score -> <score> (mediaId <id>)` plus ` + completed` when it completed.
  - Every provider error inside a job is caught per tracker and never fails the job.

Carried from `src/main/handlers/trackerHandler.ts` (`markAnilist`, `markMal`, `setAnilistProgress`, `setMalProgress`, `scoreAnilist`, `scoreMal`, `sanitizeTrackerError`), `src/main/ipc/tracker.ts` and `src/shared/hiddenMatch.ts`.

### Task 24: The franchise closure and the crawl

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/franchise/mod.rs`
- Create: `core/src/franchise/closure.rs`
- Create: `core/src/franchise/crawl.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/core.rs` (`pub(crate) crawl_recent: Mutex<HashMap<u64, Instant>>`), `core/src/lib.rs`
- Create: `core/tests/crawl.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the `anilist_media` and `relations` tables, `AnilistClient::enrichment`, `record::*`, `ImageCache`.
- Produces:
  - `closure::TRAVERSABLE: [&str; 11]` = `PREQUEL`, `SEQUEL`, `SIDE_STORY`, `SPIN_OFF`, `ALTERNATIVE`, `PARENT`, `CONTAINS`, `SUMMARY`, `COMPILATION`, `SOURCE`, `ADAPTATION`; `closure::is_traversable(relation: &str) -> bool`.
  - `closure::Node { anilist_id: u64, mal_id: Option<u64>, media_type: Option<String>, format: Option<String>, status: Option<String>, year: Option<u32>, site_url: Option<String>, title_romaji: Option<String>, title_english: Option<String>, cover_url: Option<String>, relations_fetched: bool, deferred_until: Option<i64> }`, `closure::Edge { from: u64, to: u64, relation: String }`.
  - `closure::Closure { root: u64, nodes: Vec<Node>, edges: Vec<Edge>, members: HashSet<u64>, boundary: HashSet<u64>, complete: bool, owed: Vec<u64> }`.
  - `closure::close(conn, seed: u64, cap: usize, now: i64) -> Result<Closure, CoreError>`: BFS from the seed over `relations` (`SELECT to_id, relation FROM relations WHERE from_id = ?1`), loading each node's row from `anilist_media` (a node with no row at all is drawn as a stub with only its id); the seed is a member and always drawn; a traversable edge from an expanded member makes the target a member and queues it; a node reached only across `CHARACTER` or `OTHER` is a boundary node, drawn with its edge, never expanded; nodes dedup by id and edges by `(from, to, relation)`; a member whose row has `relations_fetched_at IS NULL` is owed and never expanded past its known edges (none), and the closure is then not complete; past the cap a new node is skipped and `complete` is false; `root` is the smallest member id.
  - `closure::CAP: usize = 150`.
  - `crawl::start(core, seeds: Vec<u64>, refetch_seeds: bool) -> u64`: the Crawl job (one at a time). For each seed: `close` it; the nodes to fetch are the members whose row owes relations (`relations_fetched_at IS NULL` and `crawl_deferred_until` NULL or past) plus the seed itself when `refetch_seeds`; each fetch is `anilist.enrichment(id)`: on success, in one transaction upsert the node's own row from the enrichment fields (id, `idMal`, type, format, status, `seasonYear` else `startDate.year`, `siteUrl`, titles, cover; blanks filled, values kept, never touching `fetched_at`), replace its `relations` rows, stub every neighbour, set `relations_fetched_at = now`, `crawl_deferred_until = NULL`; then fetch the node's cover through the image cache and emit `GraphChanged { root }` at Debug; on a `Provider` error with status 429, `crawl_deferred_until = now + retry_after.unwrap_or(60)` and `deferred += 1`; on any other error, `relations_fetched_at = now` with no edges (a benign failure and an empty list look the same from outside) and a Warn line; after each fetch the closure is recomputed so new members join the walk, until nothing is owed or the cap is hit. `JobProgress` uses the crawl bar's numbers: `done` = owned AniList-matched series whose node has `relations_fetched_at`, `total` = owned AniList-matched series. Terminal `CrawlFinished { fetched, deferred }` at Info, message `crawl finished: N fetched, N deferred`.
  - `crawl::start_gap_crawl(core) -> u64`: seeds = every `series.anilist_id` whose media row owes relations, `refetch_seeds = false`; a seed whose component was closed by an earlier seed no longer owes and is skipped naturally.
  - `crawl::maybe_crawl_for_read(core, seed: u64, root: u64)`: called by Task 25's read; skips when `crawl_recent[root]` is within 60 s; else records the instant and starts `crawl::start(core, vec![seed], true)`.
  - `franchise::has_graph(conn, anilist_id) -> bool` as Task 10 defined it, moved here.

Carried from `src/shared/franchise.ts` (`closeGraph`), `src/main/services/franchiseCrawler.ts` (`REFRESH_THROTTLE_MS`, the deferral, `crawlLibraryGaps`), `src/main/services/franchiseGraph.ts` (`getFranchiseCrawlProgress`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the modules**

`closure::close`:

```rust
pub fn close(conn: &Connection, seed: u64, cap: usize, now: i64) -> Result<Closure, CoreError> {
    let mut nodes: HashMap<u64, Node> = HashMap::new();
    let mut order: Vec<u64> = Vec::new();
    let mut edges: Vec<Edge> = Vec::new();
    let mut seen_edges: HashSet<(u64, u64, String)> = HashSet::new();
    let mut members: HashSet<u64> = HashSet::new();
    let mut expanded: HashSet<u64> = HashSet::new();
    let mut owed: Vec<u64> = Vec::new();
    let mut queue: VecDeque<u64> = VecDeque::new();
    let mut hit_cap = false;

    let load = |id: u64| -> Result<Node, CoreError> { /* SELECT ... FROM anilist_media WHERE id = ?1, or a bare stub */ };
    nodes.insert(seed, load(seed)?); order.push(seed); members.insert(seed); queue.push_back(seed);

    while let Some(id) = queue.pop_front() {
        if expanded.contains(&id) { continue; }
        expanded.insert(id);
        let node = &nodes[&id];
        if !node.relations_fetched { owed.push(id); }
        let mut stmt = conn.prepare_cached("SELECT to_id, relation FROM relations WHERE from_id = ?1 ORDER BY to_id")?;
        let rels: Vec<(u64, String)> = stmt.query_map(params![id as i64], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, i64>(0)? as u64, r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        for (to, relation) in rels {
            if !nodes.contains_key(&to) {
                if nodes.len() >= cap { hit_cap = true; continue; }
                nodes.insert(to, load(to)?); order.push(to);
            }
            if seen_edges.insert((id, to, relation.clone())) { edges.push(Edge { from: id, to, relation: relation.clone() }); }
            if is_traversable(&relation) {
                members.insert(to);
                if !expanded.contains(&to) { queue.push_back(to); }
            }
        }
    }
    let boundary: HashSet<u64> = nodes.keys().filter(|id| !members.contains(id)).copied().collect();
    let root = members.iter().copied().min().unwrap_or(seed);
    let complete = !hit_cap && owed.is_empty();
    Ok(Closure { root, nodes: order.iter().map(|id| nodes[id].clone()).collect(), edges, members, boundary, complete, owed })
}
```

`deferred_until` past `now` keeps a node out of the fetch list but does not mark it owed for `complete` (it is owed; `complete` is false while any member's edges are unknown). Boundary nodes are never in `owed`.

### Task 25: The franchise layout and the graph read

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/franchise/layout.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/core.rs` (`GetFranchiseGraph` arm), `core/src/library/cards.rs` (`has_graph` through `franchise::has_graph`)
- Create: `core/tests/graph.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `closure::Closure`, `ImageCache::path_for`, the `series_anilist` index, `tracker_entries`, `crawl::maybe_crawl_for_read`.
- Produces:
  - `layout::NODE_W: f64 = 180.0`, `NODE_H: f64 = 420.0`, `SPINE_X_GAP: f64 = 320.0`, `V_GAP: f64 = 500.0`, `H_GAP: f64 = 240.0`.
  - `layout::canonical_edges(edges: &[Edge], nodes: &HashMap<u64, &Node>) -> Vec<Edge>`: the four passes of `dedupeReciprocalEdges`: (1) `PARENT` flips to a `SIDE_STORY` from parent to child; `PREQUEL` flips to a `SEQUEL` from earlier to later; `ALTERNATIVE` is ordered smaller id to larger; `ADAPTATION` and `SOURCE` are rewritten to run from print to screen and stored as `ADAPTATION`, print meaning a format in `MANGA`, `NOVEL`, `LIGHT_NOVEL`, `ONE_SHOT`, `VISUAL_NOVEL` else a media type of `MANGA` (an `ADAPTATION` from screen to print reverses; a `SOURCE` from screen to print reverses and becomes `ADAPTATION`; any other `SOURCE` becomes `ADAPTATION` in place); (2) exact duplicates drop; (3) a reciprocal drops when its kept twin exists the other way (`SOURCE` against `ADAPTATION`, `PARENT` against `SIDE_STORY`, `PREQUEL` against `SEQUEL`); (4) several edges on one ordered pair collapse to the lowest priority number: `SEQUEL`, `PREQUEL` 0; `ADAPTATION`, `SOURCE` 1; `SIDE_STORY`, `PARENT` 2; `SPIN_OFF` 3; `SUMMARY`, `COMPILATION`, `CONTAINS` 4; `ALTERNATIVE` 5; `CHARACTER` 6; `OTHER` 7; unknown 8.
  - `layout::chains(nodes, edges) -> Vec<Chain { members: HashSet<u64>, ordered: Vec<u64> }>`: connected components over `SEQUEL` edges (post-canonicalisation) of two or more nodes; each ordered by Kahn's algorithm over the internal `SEQUEL` edges with the ready set sorted by year then id; a cycle falls back to a plain year-then-id sort.
  - `layout::order_rows(chains, edges, nodes, root) -> Vec<Chain>`: the root's chain first, its parent and source chains above it in discovery order (an edge into the root that is `ADAPTATION` or `SIDE_STORY`, or out of the root that is `PARENT` or `SOURCE`, names a parent chain), then a breadth-first walk from those chains over every non-`SEQUEL` edge appending discovered chains, alternatives inserted directly below their source chain with the closest by affinity (same media type, then same format, then nearer year: `1_000_000` for a type mismatch, `1_000` for a format mismatch, plus the year gap) immediately below and farther ones cascading; unconnected chains last; with no root chain, the current series' chain is the anchor.
  - `layout::satellites(...)`: a node in no chain with a `SIDE_STORY` edge to a placed node takes the column grid of the chain holding most of its targets, centred on them, one row above when that row is free, else one row below; the surrounding rows shift by one row per satellite band; satellites sharing a target spread into neighbouring free columns. A node in no chain and with no `SIDE_STORY` edge to a placed node goes in its own row at the end, one node per column.
  - `layout::relation_label(current: u64, node: u64, chains, edges, nodes) -> Option<String>`: two nodes in one chain read `Prequel` or `Sequel` by their order; a direct edge from the current series is labelled after canonicalising against the target (`ADAPTATION` to a print target reads `Source`, `SOURCE` to a screen target reads `Adaptation`); a reverse edge is turned round first (`SOURCE` and `ADAPTATION`, `PARENT` and `SIDE_STORY`, `PREQUEL` and `SEQUEL` swap); labels `Sequel`, `Prequel`, `Parent story`, `Side story`, `Summary`, `Alternative`, `Spin-off`, `Compilation`, `Source`, `Adaptation`, `Shared characters`, `Contains`, `Other`, an unknown type lower-cased with `_` read as a space; a node with neither takes the label of the edge from its parent in the breadth-first walk.
  - `layout::layout(closure: &Closure, current: u64) -> Vec<(u64, f64, f64)>`: positions `(id, x, y)` with `x = column * SPINE_X_GAP`, `y = row * V_GAP`.
  - `franchise::graph(core, series: u64) -> Result<Option<FranchiseLayout>, CoreError>`: the series' `anilist_id` (None without one), `close`, None when the closure has one node; else `FranchiseLayout { root, nodes, edges, complete }` with each `GraphNode { anilist_id, x, y, w: NODE_W, h: NODE_H, title (romaji else english else "AniList <id>"), poster (image path when cached), owned (the series id by `anilist_id`, never for a `MANGA` node), released: status != "NOT_YET_RELEASED", format, year, relation, list_status (the AniList tracker row's status), current, root: id == root, pending: relations owed or deferred, site_url }` and the canonical edges as `GraphEdge`; then `crawl::maybe_crawl_for_read(core, anilist_id, root)` and, when any node's cover is uncached, `images::start_fill` for those URLs (a `FillImages` job with those URLs, ending in `GraphChanged { root }`).

Carried from `src/renderer/components/franchise/franchiseLayout.ts`, `laneAssignment.ts` and `FranchiseGraphView.tsx`, as the starting point the spec names, with the ghost copies and frames dropped and the rows anchored on the schema's root.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the module**

`canonical_edges` in full:

```rust
const PRINT_FORMATS: [&str; 5] = ["MANGA", "NOVEL", "LIGHT_NOVEL", "ONE_SHOT", "VISUAL_NOVEL"];

fn is_print(n: Option<&&Node>) -> bool {
    let Some(n) = n else { return false };
    n.format.as_deref().is_some_and(|f| PRINT_FORMATS.contains(&f)) || n.media_type.as_deref() == Some("MANGA")
}

fn priority(relation: &str) -> u8 {
    match relation {
        "SEQUEL" | "PREQUEL" => 0, "ADAPTATION" | "SOURCE" => 1, "SIDE_STORY" | "PARENT" => 2, "SPIN_OFF" => 3,
        "SUMMARY" | "COMPILATION" | "CONTAINS" => 4, "ALTERNATIVE" => 5, "CHARACTER" => 6, "OTHER" => 7, _ => 8,
    }
}

pub fn canonical_edges(edges: &[Edge], nodes: &HashMap<u64, &Node>) -> Vec<Edge> {
    let e = |from, to, relation: &str| Edge { from, to, relation: relation.to_string() };
    let normalized: Vec<Edge> = edges.iter().map(|x| match x.relation.as_str() {
        "PARENT" => e(x.to, x.from, "SIDE_STORY"),
        "PREQUEL" => e(x.to, x.from, "SEQUEL"),
        "ALTERNATIVE" => if x.from <= x.to { x.clone() } else { e(x.to, x.from, "ALTERNATIVE") },
        "ADAPTATION" => {
            let (fp, tp) = (is_print(nodes.get(&x.from)), is_print(nodes.get(&x.to)));
            if !fp && tp { e(x.to, x.from, "ADAPTATION") } else { x.clone() }
        }
        "SOURCE" => {
            let (fp, tp) = (is_print(nodes.get(&x.from)), is_print(nodes.get(&x.to)));
            if !fp && tp { e(x.to, x.from, "ADAPTATION") } else { e(x.from, x.to, "ADAPTATION") }
        }
        _ => x.clone(),
    }).collect();

    let mut seen = HashSet::new();
    let uniqued: Vec<Edge> = normalized.into_iter().filter(|x| seen.insert((x.from, x.to, x.relation.clone()))).collect();

    let present: HashSet<(u64, u64, String)> = uniqued.iter().map(|x| (x.from, x.to, x.relation.clone())).collect();
    let drops = |r: &str| match r { "SOURCE" => Some("ADAPTATION"), "PARENT" => Some("SIDE_STORY"), "PREQUEL" => Some("SEQUEL"), _ => None };
    let after: Vec<Edge> = uniqued.into_iter().filter(|x| !drops(&x.relation).is_some_and(|keep| present.contains(&(x.to, x.from, keep.to_string())))).collect();

    let mut best: Vec<Edge> = Vec::new();
    let mut index: HashMap<(u64, u64), usize> = HashMap::new();
    for x in after {
        match index.get(&(x.from, x.to)) {
            Some(&i) => { if priority(&x.relation) < priority(&best[i].relation) { best[i] = x; } }
            None => { index.insert((x.from, x.to), best.len()); best.push(x); }
        }
    }
    best
}
```

The rest follows the interface descriptions; the layout returns one position per node in the closure, every node placed exactly once.

  - `subscriptions::parse_output(stdout: &str) -> Result<Vec<Feed>, String>`: a JSON array of `{ name, feed_url, save_path, rule_enabled, torrent_count }`; an item without a name is dropped; `rule_enabled` defaults to true; `query` from `decode_nyaa_query(feed_url)` or empty.
  - `subscriptions::classify_failure(stderr: &str) -> SubscriptionsResult`: ANSI escapes (`\x1b[...m`) stripped, lower-cased; `cancelled` or `can't reach qbittorrent` present gives `NeedsAuth`; else `Missing`? No: else the job fails with `JobFailed { Unsupported { what: "anirss exited: <stderr>" } }`.
  - `subscriptions::start(core) -> Result<u64, CoreError>`: `Unsupported { what: "anirss on this platform" }` on Windows (`cfg!(windows)`); the Subscriptions job (one at a time) spawns `anirss -Qj` with stdin null, stdout and stderr piped, `PATH` prefixed with `~/.local/bin` when absent; waits at most 15 s, then kills with SIGTERM and ends `SubscriptionsListed { result: Timeout }`; a spawn error `NotFound` gives `Missing`; exit 0 with a JSON array gives `Ok { feeds }`; a non-zero exit gives `classify_failure`. The terminal event is `SubscriptionsListed { result }` at Debug, message `subscriptions: N feeds`, `anirss missing`, `anirss needs a qBittorrent session` or `anirss timed out`.

Carried from `src/main/handlers/subscriptionsHandler.ts` and `src/renderer/pages/SubscriptionsPage.tsx` (`decodeNyaaQuery`). Electron's ANSI regex matched a literal backslash by mistake; the native one strips real escape sequences.

### Task 28: Playback sessions and the view, mark, completion and resume rules

**Files:**
- Create: `core/src/playback/mod.rs`
- Create: `core/src/playback/session.rs`
- Modify: `core/src/core.rs` (`pub(crate) sessions: Arc<Sessions>`; arms for `OpenPlayback`, `Tick`, `ClosePlayback`, `SetTrackChoice`), `core/src/lib.rs`
- Create: `core/tests/playback.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the tables, `prefs::load_settings`, `cards::cards_for`, `writes::mark` (the Mark job), `writes::is_hidden`.
- Produces:
  - `session::Session { id: u64, file: u64, series: u64, episode_key: String, number: Option<f64>, is_extra: bool, is_film: bool, anilist_id: Option<u64>, mal_id: Option<u64>, duration: Option<f64>, outro_start: Option<f64>, last_position: Option<f64>, watched_secs: f64, viewed: bool, marked: bool, completed: bool }` and `Sessions { next: AtomicU64, map: Mutex<HashMap<u64, Session>> }`.
  - `session::VIEW_SECS: f64 = 30.0`, `MAX_TICK_DELTA: f64 = 1.5`, `MARK_FRACTION: f64 = 0.85`, `RESUME_HEAD: f64 = 5.0`, `RESUME_TAIL: f64 = 30.0`.
  - `session::open(core, file) -> Result<PlaybackSession, CoreError>`: `NotFound { File }`; loads the file and series rows, the media row, the resume point, the cached skip windows (`source != 'none'`), the sidecars, the series' `track_choice`, the settings' `subtitle_defaults`; `prev` and `next` are the neighbours among the series' on-disk episodes with integer numbers sorted by number, None for an extra or a film; `is_last_episode` is `number >= total` when a total is known, else "no next episode on disk"; `resume_from` is the stored position; `artwork` the poster path; `code` the episode code or the extra's code; `series_title` resolved per the title language; `episode_title` the airing row's title; registers the session and returns it.
  - `session::report_chapters(core, session, duration, outro_start: Option<f64>)`: Task 29 calls it to store the duration and the outro start on the session.
  - `session::tick(core, session, position, paused) -> Result<(), CoreError>`: `NotFound { Session }`; the rules, each once per session, in this order: (1) forward time: with a previous position, `delta = position - last`; `0 < delta <= MAX_TICK_DELTA` adds to `watched_secs` when not paused; (2) the view: `watched_secs >= 30`, not an extra, not yet viewed: `views` upsert by series only when `at` is newer than the stored one, `Viewed { series, episode: key }` at Info (`viewed <folder> <code>`); (3) the mark: a duration is known, the position is at or past `min(outro_start, 0.85 * duration)`, not an extra, not marked: with no ids a Warn `watched to the end but <folder> has no AniList or MAL id`; with a hidden series nothing (the guard); else `writes::mark(core, series, number)` (its refusals `NotConnected` and `NotNewer` are swallowed with a Debug line, since the rule fired once and that is the outcome); (4) completion: a duration is known and `position >= duration - 30`, or an outro start is known and `position >= outro_start`; not yet completed: delete the resume row, `ResumePointChanged { file, position: None }` at Debug, and for an episode or a film insert `completed (series, key, now)` (an extra records nothing else), then `SeriesChanged` with the card (this plan's decision); (5) the resume point: not completed, `position >= 5`: upsert `resume_points (series, key, position, duration or 0, now)` and `ResumePointChanged { file, position: Some }` at Debug. `last_position = position`.
  - `session::close(core, session, position, reason) -> Result<(), CoreError>`: applies `tick(position, paused = false)`, then for `Ended` applies completion regardless of position, then removes the session. A session that never received a tick and closes at a position under five seconds writes nothing.
  - `session::set_track_choice(core, series, audio, subtitle)`: `NotFound { Series }`; stores the JSON on the row; reply `Ok`.

Carried from `src/main/services/mpvPlayback.ts` (`MAX_TICK_DELTA_SEC`), `src/main/services/viewHistory.ts` (`markViewed`), `src/main/handlers/externalPlaybackHandler.ts` (30 s, 85 percent, the extra rule, the no-id line, the `tracked: false` rule), `src/renderer/pages/VideoPlayer.tsx` (`autoMarkAt`, the tail write, the `ended` handler, `recordEpisodeCompleted`) and `src/renderer/utils/playbackProgress.ts` (`RESUME_HEAD_SKIP`, `RESUME_TAIL_SKIP`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`core/tests/playback.rs`: fixtures with a matched series (both ids, total 12), files 1 to 3 as episodes and one extra, AniList connected with canned mark replies; (1) `OpenPlayback` on episode 2 gives `prev` = file 1, `next` = file 3, `is_last_episode: false`, `resume_from: None`, `code: "EP 2"`, the subtitle defaults; (2) 35 ticks one second apart from 0 to 34 produce `Viewed` once, `ResumePointChanged` with positions from 5 on, and `Episode.resume` afterwards; (3) a seek tick to 1200 then 1201 (duration 1400 reported through `report_chapters` with no outro) marks at 1201 (`>= 1190`): `Marked` arrives; (4) a tick at 1375 completes: `ResumePointChanged { position: None }`, `completed` holds `"2"`, `SeriesChanged` fired, `Episode.watched` true, `next_up` is file 3; (5) `ClosePlayback { Ended }` is idempotent; (6) an extra's session with 40 ticks produces no `Viewed`, no `Marked`, and completion clears its resume point only; (7) a session closed at position 3 with no prior tick leaves `resume_points` untouched; (8) `SetTrackChoice` round-trips through `OpenPlayback`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to see it fail**

Run: `cargo test -p anibeam-core --test playback`
Expected: `Unsupported`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the module**

The tick's rules as a pure function over the session plus a `TickEffects` list the caller applies, so the ordering is testable without a store:

```rust
pub enum Effect { View, Mark, Complete, Resume(f64) }

pub fn effects(s: &mut Session, position: f64, paused: bool) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    if let Some(last) = s.last_position {
        let delta = position - last;
        if !paused && delta > 0.0 && delta <= MAX_TICK_DELTA { s.watched_secs += delta; }
    }
    s.last_position = Some(position);
    if !s.viewed && !s.is_extra && s.watched_secs >= VIEW_SECS { s.viewed = true; out.push(Effect::View); }
    if !s.marked && !s.is_extra {
        let mut at: Option<f64> = s.duration.map(|d| d * MARK_FRACTION);
        if let Some(o) = s.outro_start { at = Some(at.map_or(o, |a| a.min(o))); }
        if at.is_some_and(|a| position >= a) { s.marked = true; out.push(Effect::Mark); }
    }
    let tail = s.duration.is_some_and(|d| position >= d - RESUME_TAIL);
    let outro = s.outro_start.is_some_and(|o| position >= o);
    if !s.completed && (tail || outro) { s.completed = true; out.push(Effect::Complete); }
    else if !s.completed && position >= RESUME_HEAD { out.push(Effect::Resume(position)); }
    out
}
```

`close` with `Ended` pushes `Complete` when not yet completed. A mark from the rule runs `writes::mark` as a job; the session's `marked` flag is set when the rule fires, not when the tracker answers.

Expected: pass.

```bash
git commit -m "feat(core): playback sessions with the view, mark, completion and resume rules"
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the tables, `Secrets`, `accounts`, `prefs`, `cards::cards_for`, `scan` (an import creates missing series and unavailable sources; the launch scan attaches them).
- Produces:
  - `format::VERSION: u32 = 2`, `format::FORMAT: &str = "anibeam-export"`.
  - `format::Document` with serde `rename_all = "camelCase"`: `format`, `version`, `exported_at: String`, `exported_by: ExportedBy { app, line, version }`, `private: bool`, `sources: Vec<SourceEntry { path }>`, `series: Vec<SeriesEntry { kind: String ("series" | "movie"), path, id: String, title: String, hidden: bool, match_: Option<MatchEntry> (rename "match"), track_choice: Option<TrackChoice> (version 2, `skip_serializing_if` None) }>`, `accounts: Option<Accounts { main: String, anilist: Option<Account>, mal: Option<Account> }>`, `keys: Option<serde_json::Value>`, `history: Option<History { views: Vec<View { series, last_episode: f64, at }>, completed: Vec<Completed { series, episode: f64, at }>, resume_points: Vec<ResumeEntry> }>`, `preferences: Option<PrefsEntry { title_language, library_tab, library_sort: SortEntry { key, direction }, feed_sort, auto_skip: Option<AutoSkip> }>`. `MatchEntry` is an untagged enum of `{ provider: "anilist" | "mal", anilist_id: Option<u64>, mal_id: Option<u64> }` and `{ provider: "tmdb", tmdb_id: u64, tmdb_kind: String }`. `ResumeEntry` is untagged: `{ series, episode: f64, position, duration, at }` or `{ file, position, duration, at }`. `Account { user_id: Option<u64>, username: Option<String>, client_id: String, client_secret: Option<String>, access_token: String, refresh_token: Option<String>, expires_at: Option<serde_json::Value> }` (Electron writes a millisecond epoch number or null; the native export writes an ISO string; the reader accepts both).
  - `format::parse(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(Document, Vec<String>), CoreError>`: `Invalid { field: "format" }` when `format` is not `anibeam-export`; `Version { found, supported: 2 }` when `version > 2`; unknown top-level and per-entry fields are collected by name into the returned list (deserialise into `serde_json::Value` first, walk the known keys, then into `Document` with `#[serde(flatten)] extra: HashMap<String, Value>` on each struct); version 1 migrates in memory by leaving the version 2 fields None.
  - `format::parse_instant(s: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<i64>`: an RFC 3339 string, or a millisecond epoch number.
  - `export::build(core, private: bool, now) -> Result<Document, CoreError>`: `exported_by = { app: "anibeam", line: "native", version: VERSION }`; sources in id order; every series (missing included) with its match (only the provider it carries), `hidden`, `track_choice`; with `private`: accounts (tokens and the client secret from the secrets store, `expires_at` as an ISO string, a disconnected tracker as null), `keys: {}`, history (`views` as `{ series: path, last_episode: number or 0 for a film, at }`, `completed` one per row, `resume_points` by series and episode for an episode key that parses as a number, else by file path), preferences with `auto_skip`.
  - `export::start(core, path, private) -> u64`: the Export job writes the document as pretty JSON through a `.tmp` sibling and rename; terminal `ExportFinished { path }` at Info, message `exported N series to <path>`.
  - `import::start(core, path) -> Result<u64, CoreError>`: reads and parses synchronously so a newer file is `Err(Version)` at once and an unreadable file `Err(Io)`; the Import job applies, in one transaction per section: sources upserted by path with `available` from the disk (`sources_added` counts new rows, `sources_unavailable` the unavailable ones); series upserted by `(kind, path)` under the source whose path contains it, longest prefix, existing or imported (an entry under no source is skipped and its path counted under `fields_ignored` as `series[<n>].path`), the file's `hidden` and `track_choice` winning, `missing_since = now` for a path not on disk (only for new rows; an existing row keeps its state), the match columns with `confirmed = 1` and `matched_at = now` (`matches_applied` counts entries with a match) and a media stub for an AniList match; history: `views` by path, newer `at` wins (`views_merged` counts rows written), `completed` by path and number, newer wins, `resume_points` by series and episode key `<number>`, or by file: the owning series is the one whose path equals the file (a film) or is the longest prefix of it (a show), with the file name as the key; accounts: the row upserted (`user_id`, `username`, `client_id`, `expires_at`, `connected_at = now` when a token exists), tokens and the secret to the secrets store with `secret_store` recorded (`accounts_imported` counts trackers with a token); `main` to the main tracker setting; `keys.tmdb` and every unknown field counted in `fields_ignored`; preferences (and `auto_skip` when present) to the settings table. Events: `JobProgress` per section, `SourceChanged` per source, `SeriesChanged` for every touched series, `TrackersChanged`, `PreferencesChanged`, terminal `ImportFinished { summary }` at Info, message `imported: N sources, N series, N matches, N views, N completed, N resume points, N accounts, N fields ignored`. Importing the same file twice reports zero changes (every count 0 except `fields_ignored`).

### Task 31: The launch sequence, shutdown, the CLI views and the exit check

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything.
- Produces:
  - `launch::start(core: &Arc<Core>) -> Result<(), CoreError>`, called by `Core::start`: (1) emit `Ready`; (2) build the watcher and watch every available source; (3) start the catch-up `scan::start(core, ScanScope::All)`; (4) register a one-shot listener on the bus that, on that scan's `Finished` event, starts in this order `automatch::start`, `apply::backfill_stubs`, `crawl::start_gap_crawl`, `airing::start_refresh_library`, `cache::start_refresh(None, force = false)`, `images::start_fill`, and runs `images.sweep(now)` on the runtime; (5) the Info line `secrets: keyring unavailable, using secrets.json` when the keyring was not constructed. Nothing else runs on a timer, ever.
  - `Core::shutdown`: idempotent: cancel every job, drop the watcher, wait up to two seconds for the runtime's tasks to observe cancellation, checkpoint, close the store.
  - The dispatch: every `Call` variant has an arm; the catch-all `Unsupported` arm is deleted and the compiler proves the coverage.
  - CLI: `anibeam-cli jobs` (the `ListJobs` reply as a table), `anibeam-cli import <file> [--wait]` and `anibeam-cli export <file> [--private] [--wait]` as shortcuts to `call`.
  - `scripts/electron-list.mjs`: reads the Electron app's `config.json` and `metadata.json` from `~/.config/anibeam/` (or `ANIBEAM_ELECTRON_DATA`), reproduces the Home grid's All tab in alpha order with the JP title rule (romaji, english, folder name; hidden series excluded), and prints one title per line. Copy `pickTitle` and the tab rule from the digest of `HomePage.tsx` and `TitleLanguageContext.tsx`; the file is a plain bun script with no imports from `src/`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Run the phase 1 exit check on the real library**

The check, on the desktop, against the packaged Electron app's data (the Electron app may stay closed or open; the native core reads its own sandbox):

```bash
# 1. The Electron side: export the library from Settings > Export (unticked) to ~/anibeam-export.json,
#    and print the grid as Electron shows it.
bun scripts/electron-list.mjs > /tmp/electron-list.txt

# 2. The native side, in a sandbox so the real XDG directories stay untouched.
cargo build --release -p anibeam-cli
CLI="target/release/anibeam-cli --root /tmp/anibeam-exit-check"
$CLI import ~/anibeam-export.json --wait
$CLI scan --wait
$CLI list --tab all --sort alpha --direction asc | awk -F'\t' '{ print $3 }' > /tmp/native-list.txt

# 3. Compare.
diff /tmp/electron-list.txt /tmp/native-list.txt && echo "phase 1 exit: identical"
```

`list` prints tab-separated columns `id`, `code`, `title`, `watched/total`, so the third column is the title. The import carries every match, so the titles come from the same AniList records; the scan attaches the files. A difference is a bug in the walk, the classifier, the title rule or the sort, and it is fixed before this task closes. Record the run (the date, the machine, the series count and `identical`) in the commit message.
